However, existing large language model (LLM)-based scientific agents still face challenges in autonomous planning of complex tasks, adaptive selection of specialized models, and reliable execution of multi-stage scientific computations, limiting their effectiveness in automating complex drug discovery workflows. To address these challenges, we propose DrugCoding, a scientific programming agent system that integrates scientific task planning, adaptive model routing, multi-agent collaboration, and programmatic tool execution. DrugCoding enables autonomous task planning and dynamic model adaptation for complex research objectives. It can flexibly invoke specialized scientific models, autonomously generate and execute code according to task requirements, and leverage multi-agent collaboration to support efficient execution and reliable validation of complex scientific tasks. To evaluate DrugCoding's performance in scientific tool invocation and multi-stage task execution, we conduct multi-turn tool-calling experiments across diverse language models and scientific tool interfaces. Experimental results demonstrate that DrugCoding achieves an average session-level Pass@1 of 97.35% on the full benchmark, outperforming the strongest baseline, Codex, by 9.24%. On the challenging subset, DrugCoding achieves 97.92%, exceeding the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [68] [Benchmarking LLM Next-Step Recommendations with Knowledge Space Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13667)
*Peter Steiner,Jan Hochweber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以知识空间理论（KST）为形式化评价参考的基准方法，用于评估LLM在"下一步学习推荐"任务上的结构准确性，并在四个数学领域、六模型、四种提示条件下开展样例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为"下一步推荐"选择LLM需要能反映目标教育任务与领域的基准；现有评价缺乏与任务领域相关的形式化参照系，难以判断模型推荐的结构正确性。

Method: 以KST作为形式化评价参考：给定任务目录与已有先修关系，推导可容许知识状态及每个状态下完整的即时下一步集合；将模型在指定提示下生成的推荐与这些参考集合精确比对。样例研究使用六个模型、四个数学领域、四种提示条件、两种目录顺序，共2400条响应。

Result: 精确集合准确率：仅提供描述61.3%，要求模型重建先修关系62.8%，提供先修关系96.3%，提供完整知识空间98.2%；性能随模型与领域变化；两种呈现顺序下推荐集合一致率为85.25%。

Conclusion: 该基准支持将模型—提示组合与情境特定参考进行对比；其分数仅描述所评估条件下的结构推荐准确性，不能证明学习收益，也不能证明使用LLM进行选择的必要性。

Abstract: Selecting an LLM for next-step recommendation requires benchmarks that reflect the intended educational task and domain. We present a benchmarking method that uses Knowledge Space Theory (KST) as a formal evaluation reference. Given a task catalogue and an existing prerequisite relation, the method derives admissible knowledge states and the complete set of immediate next steps for each state. Recommendations generated by a model under a specified prompt are compared with these reference sets. A sample evaluation study uses 2,400 responses from six models across four mathematics domains, four prompt conditions, and two catalogue orders. Exact-set accuracy was 61.3% with descriptions only, 62.8% when models were asked to reconstruct prerequisites, 96.3% with supplied prerequisite relations, and 98.2% with the complete knowledge space. Performance varied across models and domains, and recommendation sets agreed across the two presentations in 85.25% of pairs. The benchmark supports comparisons of model-prompt combinations against a context-specific reference. Its scores describe structural recommendation accuracy under the evaluated conditions; they do not establish learning benefit or the necessity of using an LLM for selection.

</details>


### [69] [A computational framework for understanding embodied plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13669)
*Aalap D. Shah,Basil Saeed,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Tobias Gerstenberg,Hyowon Gweon,Ilker Yildirim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenEA：一种基于多层级状态空间与生成模型的行为体表征，通过扩展任务与运动规划并耦合直觉物理，可对长时序多步具身计划进行推理，在操作与导航任务中复现人类计划推理并优于多种强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从他人行为推断其计划与目标是人类社会互动的基础，也是合作型具身人工智能的关键能力；但在自然、具身情境中对这一能力进行形式化建模长期存在挑战，尤其缺乏能处理多步、长时序具身计划的表征方法。

Method: 提出GenEA智能体表征：构建多层级状态空间，耦合铰接身体的粗略生物力学（姿态/运动）与与近邻物体的交互，从而将机器人学中的任务与运动规划（TAMP）框架与基于仿真的直觉物理相结合；再将该状态空间嵌入生成模型，得到关于目标与具身计划的联合概率分布；通过对该联合分布的简单变换（如条件化、边缘化）实现不同推理模式。

Result: 在物体操作与导航两个性质不同的领域、涵盖多样任务与多种行为测量的实验中，GenEA能够复现人类的具身计划推理模式，并稳定优于强替代模型，包括合理的启发式策略、标准任务与运动规划方法，以及先进的视觉-语言模型。

Conclusion: GenEA为人类推理与社会认知的架构提供了新视角，表明计划推理可被理解为在具身多层级状态空间上的生成式概率推断；并提示希望与人类无缝交互的具身智能系统宜采用GenEA式生成式动作规划模型。

Abstract: Reasoning about people's plans and goals based on their actions is fundamental to human social interaction and essential for cooperative, embodied artificial intelligence. Formalizing this capacity in naturalistic, embodied settings has been a longstanding challenge. Here, we present GenEA, a novel agent representation capable of reasoning over multi-step, long-horizon embodied plans. At the heart of GenEA lies a multilevel state-space that couples the coarse biomechanics of an articulated body and its interactions with nearby objects by extending the task-and-motion planning framework from robotics with simulation-based intuitive physics. By embedding this state-space in a generative model, GenEA yields a joint probability distribution over goals and embodied plans. Across several experiments spanning the qualitatively distinct domains of object manipulation and navigation, featuring diverse tasks and multiple behavioral measures, we show that GenEA, through simple transformations of this joint distribution, recapitulates how humans reason about embodied plans. Crucially, GenEA consistently outperforms strong alternative models, including plausible heuristics, standard task-and-motion planning, and a state-of-the-art vision-language model. GenEA thereby offers a new perspective on the architecture of human reasoning and social cognition, suggesting that embodied artificial intelligence systems seeking to seamlessly interact with humans should adopt GenEA-like generative models of action planning.

</details>


### [70] [Topological Dynamics Under Non-Stationary Supervision: Persistent Homology of Neural Network Weight Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13701)
*Mitra Varun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用15个共享初始化与批次顺序的MLP，在Fashion-MNIST上分别在正确标签、固定随机置换标签、每轮随机置换标签三种监督下训练，采集22个检查点的神经元权重点云并计算H0/H1 Vietoris-Rips持续同调，检验拓扑特征能否刻画监督信号结构及其与泛化能力的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经元权重点云的持续拓扑在"稳定监督"与"非平稳监督"下如何演化，并检验拓扑特征相对常规诊断指标能否增量地预测训练条件（进而反映泛化）。

Method: 相同结构的多层感知机，三种标签条件（正确标签、固定随机置换、每轮新置换）；5对配对种子共享初始化与minibatch顺序，共15个模型、训练100轮；在22个检查点上对原始与行归一化的入边权重点云计算H0、H1 Vietoris-Rips持续同调（共3300个拓扑样本）；采用精确比较并做Holm校正，并做早期条件分类与常规诊断的对照。

Result: 正确标签模型平均真标签测试准确率89.39%；固定随机标签模型仅拟合其指定目标的67.94%且不泛化到原任务。正确标签模型的时间平均归一化fc3 H1持续同调在描述上高于两种随机标签条件，但配置的精确比较经Holm校正后未达常规显著性。早期条件分类平衡准确率：仅拓扑60.0%、仅常规诊断66.7%、二者合并60.0%，即无增量预测提升。

Conclusion: 在该设定下权重点云拓扑特征未提供超越常规诊断的增量预测能力，拓扑与监督结构间的关系仅为描述性趋势；结论受5个种子、单一架构与数据集、282个被封顶的H1距离比较以及尚未完成的瓶颈分析所限。

Abstract: We investigate how the persistent topology of neuron- weight point clouds evolves under stable and non-stationary supervision. An identical multilayer perceptron is trained on Fashion-MNIST with correct labels, a fixed random permutation of the training-label vector, or a fresh permutation every epoch. Five paired seeds share initialisation and minibatch order, yielding 15 models trained for 100 epochs. Raw and row-normalised incoming weight clouds are analysed with H0 and H1 Vietoris Rips persistence at 22 checkpoints. Correct-label models reach 89.39% mean true-label test accuracy; fixed-random models fit 67.94% of their assigned training targets without generalising to the original task. Correct-label models have descriptively greater time-averaged normalised fc3 H1 persistence than either random-label condition, but the configured exact comparisons do not reach conventional significance after Holm correction. Early condition classification achieves 60.0% mean balanced accuracy using topology, 66.7% using conventional diagnostics, and 60.0% using both. Thus, no incremental predictive improvement is demonstrated in this setting. The archive contains all 3,300 configured topology cases. Interpretation is limited by five seeds, a single architecture and dataset, 282 capped H1 distance comparisons, and deferred bottleneck analysis.

</details>


### [71] [SeqFold: Timestamp-Addressable Compressed States for User Action Sequence Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13704)
*Mianwei Zhou,Duo Zhang,Henry Tsai,Yuqi Zheng,Jian Li,Juexin Lin,Sujay Khandagale,Vijay Rajaram,Dong Jiao,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SeqFold，一种压缩状态接口，将冻结长历史序列骨干网络的多层用户状态导出为紧凑、可按时间戳寻址的离散表示，供下游模型回填与在线服务复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列用户建模多聚焦于改进骨干编码器或为单一预测目标学习任务专属表示，缺乏对冻结长历史骨干输出状态的高效复用机制；下游系统实际只需对齐到目标时间戳的状态而非完整隐状态时间线。

Method: SeqFold 组合三部分：可学习的多通道融合、通道特定的时间衰减、无查找量化（Lookup-Free Quantization, LFQ），从冻结骨干状态生成紧凑离散码；再由任务专属解码器将共享码映射到灵活嵌入空间。同时提出融合流式实现，在每个行为后更新融合递推、仅在目标时间戳物化状态，以实现规模化时间戳可寻址导出。

Result: 在大型专有数据集上，单状态表示存储降低约 3,400 倍，同时保留未压缩模型大部分下游排序性能。

Conclusion: SeqFold 可作为复用长历史序列骨干用于下游模型的有效接口，在存储与性能间取得显著折衷。

Abstract: Existing sequential user modeling methods mainly focus on improving the backbone encoder or learning task-specific user representations for a single prediction objective. We instead study how to export the multi-layer user states of a frozen long-history backbone as compact, timestamp-addressable representations for downstream training backfill and serving. In this setting, the user state at a given timestamp summarizes the user's actions before that time, so downstream systems need only the state aligned to each target timestamp rather than the full hidden-state timeline. To this end, we present \texttt{SeqFold}, a compressed-state interface that combines learned multi-channel fusion, channel-specific temporal decay, and Lookup-Free Quantization (LFQ) to produce compact discrete codes from frozen backbone states. Task-specific decoders then map these shared codes into flexible embedding spaces for downstream models. To make timestamp-addressable export practical at scale, we further develop a fused streaming implementation that updates the fusion recurrence after every action while materializing states only at target timestamps. On large-scale proprietary datasets, SeqFold reduces per-state representation storage by $3{,}400\times$ while preserving most of the downstream ranking performance of the uncompressed model, showing that SeqFold is an effective interface for reusing long-history sequence backbones in downstream models.

</details>


### [72] [Agentic AI Meets Formal Verification in the Era of Agentic Hardware Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13705)
*Wenji Fang,Yuchao Wu,Jing Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是一篇邀请综述，系统梳理AI时代硬件形式化方法的双向互动：AI（尤其智能体）如何推进形式化方法，以及形式化方法如何保障AI驱动的硬件开发，并展望"证明原生"硬件开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体已能生成并优化硬件电路，其设计正确性验证愈发关键；同时AI降低了形式化方法的使用门槛，二者结合存在待梳理的新机遇。

Method: 综述性方法论梳理：从规约、证明构造、推理引擎三个层次分析智能体AI对硬件形式化方法的推进；并从RTL生成、调试、保正确性优化三方面讨论形式化方法对AI硬件开发的支持；结合代表性研究与作者近期工作。

Result: 归纳了各层面的关键技术进展，并总结实验得出的洞察（摘要未给出具体量化结果）。

Conclusion: 提出面向"证明原生硬件开发"的未来研究方向，使形式化推理成为自主硬件设计的组成部分。

Abstract: As AI agents become increasingly capable of generating and optimizing hardware circuits, ensuring the correctness of AI-generated designs becomes more important. Meanwhile, advances in AI lower the barriers to developing and applying formal methods, creating new opportunities to automate and improve hardware verification. This invited paper presents an overview of the methodologies and applications of hardware formal methods in the AI era. First, we examine how agentic AI advances hardware formal methods across three levels: specification, proof construction, and reasoning engines. Second, we discuss how formal methods support reliable AI-driven hardware development through RTL generation, debugging, and correctness-preserving optimization. We review representative research alongside our recent contributions, highlighting key technical advances and insights from experimental results. Finally, we discuss future research opportunities toward proof-native hardware development, where formal reasoning could become an integral part of autonomous hardware design.

</details>


### [73] [AutoManifold: Agentic Design of Data Visualisation Algorithms via Manifold Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13710)
*Burak Susam,Ruiyuan Kang,Tingting Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AutoManifold：一个多智能体 LLM 驱动的流形嵌入算法自动生成流水线，可根据用户指定的结构保持偏好合成新算法，在真实数据集上优于 t-SNE、UMAP、PaCMAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形嵌入 SOTA（t-SNE、UMAP、PaCMAP）均为手工设计，设计目标在开发时固定，行为不灵活，难以按用户指定的结构优先级调节；而手工设计新算法需要高度专业知识且耗时。因此需要面向用户偏好的自主算法设计方法。

Method: AutoManifold 在受限原语词表（affinity、cost、optimisation）上组合新嵌入算法，由多智能体 LLM 编排；设计各阶段均以用户指定的结构保持偏好为条件，并通过 LLM 引导、指标驱动的迭代闭环精化算法配置。

Result: 在覆盖不同难度区间的真实数据集上、统一评测框架下与 t-SNE、UMAP、PaCMAP 三种最强常用 SOTA 对比，生成的算法在向用户指定属性引导的能力上更优，且不损害原始数据的内在结构。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的偏好条件化算法合成可超越超参数调优，产生真正的新算法；代码已开源（GitHub: buraksusam/AutoManifold_NeurIPS2026）。

Abstract: Manifold embedding for data visualisation is dominated by a small set of hand-designed state-of-the-art (SOTA) algorithms. Their design targets are fixed at development time, resulting in inflexible algorithmic behaviour that cannot be readily steered towards user-specified structural priorities. Hand-designing new embedding algorithms to address user priority requires highly specialised expertise and is time consuming. To address autonomous algorithm design tailored to user preference, we introduce an agentic algorithm generation pipeline AutoManifold. It composes new manifold embedding algorithms from a constrained vocabulary of affinity, cost, and optimisation primitives, supported by multi-agent large-language-model (LLM) orchestration. AutoManifold conditions every stage of its design on user-specified structural preservation preferences, and iteratively refines the algorithm configuration through an LLM-guided, metric-grounded iterative loop. We compare the generated algorithms against the three strongest and most frequently used SOTA methods (t-SNE, UMAP, and PaCMAP) on real-world datasets spanning different difficulty regimes under identical evaluation infrastructure. The results demonstrate that LLM-driven, priority-conditioned algorithm synthesis can move beyond hyperparameter tuning, producing genuinely new algorithms that outperform traditional hand-designed methods in their ability to steer towards user-specified properties without compromising the intrinsic structure in the original data. The code of AutoManifold is available at https://github.com/buraksusam/AutoManifold_NeurIPS2026.

</details>


### [74] [Broad-Persistent Advising in Interactive Reinforcement Learning via Latent State Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13712)
*Zhuoran Liang,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Encoder BPA，用训练好的编码器将状态序列映射为潜在表示，通过潜在比较、top-k 聚合与一致性条件实现建议的检索与复用，在复杂和部分可观测环境中提升训练效率与建议利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Deep IntRL 仅在当前步使用建议，交互一次后即丢弃；Persistent Advising 与 BPA 虽通过持久记忆和基于 K-means 聚类的状态泛化复用建议，但其状态泛化方式存在局限，难以在复杂、部分可观测环境中稳定检索到相关建议。

Method: 在每个时间步用已训练的编码器将状态序列映射为潜在表示；通过潜在空间的相似度比较检索建议，并引入 top-k 聚合与一致性条件来提升复用稳定性。

Result: 实验表明，与不同 Deep IntRL 方法相比，Encoder BPA 在训练早期阶段提升了学习效率和建议利用率，尤其在复杂及部分可观测环境中效果更明显。

Conclusion: 以学习到的潜在表示替代基于聚类的状态泛化进行建议检索与复用，是提升 Deep IntRL 建议利用效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Deep Interactive Reinforcement Learning (Deep IntRL) accelerates reinforcement learning training by incorporating external advice. However, conventional Deep IntRL uses each piece of advice only in the current step and discards it after a single interaction. Persistent Advising and Broad-Persistent Advising (BPA) extend this process by storing advice in persistent memory and reusing it in similar states through state generalisation based on K-means clustering. This research proposes Encoder BPA, which employs a latent encoder to retrieve and reuse advice. In this framework, a trained encoder is used to map a state sequence into a latent representation at every time step. Advice can be retrieved through latent comparison, together with a top-(k) aggregation and a consistency condition to improve reuse stability. Experimental results show that Encoder BPA improves learning efficiency and advice utilisation at an early stage of training compared with different Deep IntRL approaches, especially in complex and partially observable environments.

</details>


### [75] [Toward Autonomous RTL and Synthesis Optimization: Benchmarking and Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13713)
*Wenji Fang,Yao Lu,Jinghua Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向自主RTL与综合优化的三个关联方向：真实基准评测、基于工具驱动的多智能体框架Dr. RTL，以及设计特定的综合优化，并展望了AI原生设计自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM与自主智能体的进展为硬件设计优化带来新机遇，但现有工作缺乏贴近真实EDA流程的评测基准，且RTL优化与综合优化尚未有效衔接。

Method: (1) 构建基于参考与开放式的RTL优化基准，在真实EDA工作流下评估优化机会；(2) 提出多智能体框架Dr. RTL，自动化关键路径分析、RTL重写与实现级评估，并通过可复用的优化技能持续改进；(3) 探索智能体驱动的综合脚本调优及可执行综合算法发现，覆盖中间表示、与工艺无关优化和工艺映射。

Result: 在20个真实RTL设计上，Dr. RTL相较商用综合实现WNS/TNS平均提升21%/17%，面积减少6%。

Conclusion: 作者展望AI原生的设计自动化，在验证约束与实现反馈的引导下，联合探索体系结构、RTL与EDA算法设计空间。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) and autonomous agents are creating new opportunities for hardware design optimization. This paper examines three connected directions toward autonomous RTL and synthesis optimization: (1) Realistic benchmarking. We discuss reference-based and open-ended RTL optimization benchmarks that evaluate optimization opportunities under realistic EDA workflows. (2) Tool-grounded RTL optimization. We present Dr. RTL, a multi-agent framework that automates critical-path analysis, RTL rewriting, and implementation-level evaluation, while continuously improving through reusable optimization skills. Evaluated on 20 real-world RTL designs, Dr.~RTL achieves average WNS/TNS improvements of 21\%/17\% with a 6\% area reduction over commercial synthesis. (3) Design-specific synthesis optimization. We explore agent-driven synthesis script tuning and the discovery of executable synthesis algorithms across intermediate representations, technology-independent optimization, and technology mapping. Finally, we envision AI-native design automation that jointly explores architectural, RTL, and EDA algorithm design spaces, guided by verification constraints and implementation feedback.

</details>


### [76] [Integrating Action Prefix Prediction and Alignment Across Modalities for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13782)
*Sijie Xu,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出改进预测表征（IPR），以观测锚点+动作前缀直接预测未来表征，并做观测锚点对齐，在DMC多任务上平均回报优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态表征学习在交互数据有限的强化学习中困难。现有MJPR将多模态隐预测与跨模态对比学习结合，但其递归rollout依赖前序预测、逐对对齐只关注模态间对应关系，存在误差累积与对齐目标偏差。

Method: 基于对比多模态学习，在每个预测horizon上直接从观测锚点和对应动作序列预测未来表征，避免递归预测依赖；对齐发生在观测锚点而非模态对之间。设置两种裁剪配置：IPR-S（最小保留裁剪面积10%）与IPR-H（50%）。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite五个任务上，IPR-H平均回报比基线：视觉+深度图像高10.0%，视觉图像+位置向量高3.4%；两种配置相对表现因任务而异。Webots中，无LiDAR时两种IPR平均最终回报均高于基线，有LiDAR时不优于基线。

Conclusion: 以观测锚点为基准的直接多步预测表征在有限数据下更有效，但增益依赖模态组合与任务特性（如LiDAR存在时优势消失），裁剪强度需按任务选择。

Abstract: Learning representations from multimodal observations is difficult in reinforcement learning with limited interaction data. Multimodal Joint Predictive Representation combines multimodal latent prediction with contrastive learning across modalities, but its recursive rollout depends on earlier predictions and its pairwise alignment focuses on correspondence between modalities. We propose Improved Predictive Representation, which combines prediction from action prefixes with alignment at the observed anchor based on Contrastive Multimodal learning. At each horizon, it predicts future representations directly from the observed anchor and the corresponding sequence of actions. We evaluate two crop configurations: IPR-S with a minimum retained crop area of 10%, and IPR-H with 50%. Across five DeepMind Control Suite tasks, IPR-H achieves an average return across tasks that is 10.0% higher than the baseline with visual and depth images and 3.4% higher with visual images and position vectors. The relative performance of the two configurations varies across tasks. In Webots, both IPR configurations achieve higher mean final returns than the baseline without LiDAR, but not when LiDAR is available.

</details>


### [77] [Ceci n'est pas une pipe -- Toward a Symbolic Solver for the Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus Using Evolutionary Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13831)
*Luca Favole,Giorgio Mongardi,Giovanni Squillero,Alberto Tonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个提交至ARC-AGI-2主竞赛的纯符号进化式程序合成方法：将网格解析器与输出投影器同程序联合进化，以涌现任务特定语言，并用核心知识理论基定解析符号，配合对象感知适应度提升搜索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC基准用于评估AI从少量样本泛化的能力，而进化算法（EA）虽长期成功，却缺席ARC-AGI主竞赛。作为“评分-搜索”式程序合成方法，EA面临两大难题：一是难以选定合适的领域特定语言表示（像素级或对象级），二是难以区分产生相同输出的候选解。

Method: 提出全符号化的进化程序合成框架，三项关键设计：(1) 将网格解析器（parser）与输出投影器（projector）同变换程序一并进化，使任务特定语言在合成过程中涌现；(2) 将解析器可提取的符号基于认知心理学的核心知识理论进行基定；(3) 设计对象感知适应度，超越像素级差异对部分正确输出进行分级评价。

Result: 该方法作为首个纯符号进化式程序合成方案被提交至ARC-AGI-2主竞赛，验证了EA在ARC任务上的可行路径；抽象未给出具体得分数据。

Conclusion: 通过联合进化表示与程序、以认知理论约束符号空间、并采用对象级适应度，可缓解EA在表示选择与解区分度上的固有缺陷，为符号进化方法参与ARC类抽象推理任务提供了可行范式。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) is a benchmark designed to assess the capability of Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems to generalize starting from a few samples, a task humans excel at. The ARC-AGI contests held since 2020 have been the focus of intense competition, with participants proposing approaches ranging from traditional symbolic AI to Transformer architectures. Despite a long history of successes, evolutionary algorithms (EAs) were notably absent from the approaches appearing in the main contest. As score-and-search program synthesis approaches, EAs suffer from issues related to choosing the proper representation in the Domain-Specific Language applied to the problem (for example, pixel-level or object-level), and distinguishing between two candidate solutions producing the same outputs. In this work, we present the first fully symbolic, evolutionary-based program synthesis approach submitted to the ARC-AGI-2 main contest. The main contributions are: evolving the grid parser and the output projector together with the program that transforms the grid, so that a task-specific language emerges during program synthesis; grounding the symbols the parser can extract in core knowledge theory from cognitive psychology; and an object-aware fitness that grades partially correct outputs beyond pixel-level discrepancies.

</details>


### [78] [Taxonomy-Agnostic Neurosymbolic Grounding of Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13850)
*Bastien Dussard,Aur{é}lie Clodic,Guillaume Sarthou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: URDF2Ontology 是一个自动化、与分类体系无关的神经符号框架，将原始 URDF 文件转换为填充并落地的 OWL 本体，用于机器人任务分配中的符号推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号化任务分配需要将机器人物理能力落地为形式化语义，但标准 URDF 仅提供底层运动学信息，缺乏推理所需的形式化语义上下文，且人工构建领域本体成本高。

Method: 框架利用 LLM 进行定向语义推理，配合迭代式序列图剪枝机制防止上下文窗口饱和；针对复杂运动学结构提出形态感知双轨架构：自底向上的树轨处理分支装配，自顶向下的链轨处理串联肢体；在神经符号护栏引导下抽取功能子图，并按用户任意给定分类体系断言生成填充本体。

Result: 在异构机器人机队上的实证评估表明，该方法具有强模式对齐、有效的结构抽取，并显著降低人工领域建模开销。

Conclusion: URDF2Ontology 实现了从 URDF 到可推理 OWL 本体的自动化、分类体系无关的转换，为机器人任务分配提供了可扩展的语义基础。

Abstract: While symbolic reasoning for task allocation requires grounding a robot's physical capabilities, standard Unified Robot Description Format (URDF) files provide only low-level kinematics without the formal semantic context needed for reasoning. We present URDF2Ontology, an automated, taxonomy-agnostic neurosymbolic framework that transforms raw URDF files into populated, grounded OWL ontologies. It leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) for targeted semantic reasoning, coupled with an iterative sequential graph-purging mechanism to prevent context window saturation. To process complex kinematic structures, it employs a morphology-aware dual-track architecture featuring a bottom-up Tree Track for branching assemblies and a top-down Chain Track for serial limbs. Guided by neurosymbolic guardrails, the framework extracts functional sub-graphs and asserts populated ontologies aligned with arbitrary user-provided taxonomies. Empirical evaluation across a diverse fleet of heterogeneous robots demonstrates strong schema alignment, valid structural extraction, and a significant reduction in manual domain authoring overhead.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond Proxy Metrics: MLLMs as Human Surrogates for Explainable AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13926)
*Eoin M. Kenny,Salim I. Amoukou,Tom Bewley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）作为"代理人类被试"来自动化XAI解释评估，提出"目的锚定"评价框架，并在14个MLLM与5类目的场景上验证OpenAI与Qwen系列对齐度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI解释方法评估长期困难：代理指标难以反映解释的实际效用，而人类评估虽被视为金标准，却成本高、难规模化、难以复现。

Method: 提出"purpose-grounded"评价框架，其指标同时适用于MLLM与真实用户；给出为何、何时可信任MLLM代替人类进行评估的概念性论证；并开展覆盖5个目的锚定场景（如模型改进）与14个主流MLLM的实证测试。

Result: 所有被测模型均呈正向趋势；OpenAI与Qwen系列模型与其他变体相比对齐度普遍更高，是实践中推荐的选择。

Conclusion: MLLM可作为XAI评估的补充手段，而非替代用户测试，帮助研究者摆脱对代理指标的过度依赖。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) has expanded into a broad and impactful research area, but the fundamental question of how best to evaluate explanation techniques has proven frustratingly elusive. Methodologies typically revolve around either proxy metrics which tell us little about the utility of an explanation, or human evaluation which---while generally considered the gold standard---is expensive, difficult to scale, and challenging to reproduce. In this paper, we investigate if Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can act as surrogate human participants to automate the evaluation of XAI. To do this, we first propose a ``purpose-grounded'' evaluation framework with metrics applicable to both MLLMs and human users alike. Then, we offer some conceptual insights as to why and when we can trust these models to automate this process. Lastly, we conduct extensive empirical testing across five purpose-grounded areas such as model improvement and fourteen popular MLLMs. Results indicate that all models trend positively, but OpenAI and Qwen models generally show higher alignment compared to other variants, which would be the recommended choices in practice. We expect our insights not to replace user testing, but rather to help XAI researchers complement their current evaluation methodologies and move the field away from heavy reliance on proxy metrics for measuring explanation utility.

</details>


### [80] [TreeAgent: Structure-Aware Memory Agent for Processing Long Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13946)
*Wei Gu,Weiwei Qi,Jiajun Tang,Tianhang Zheng,Bo Chen,Zhibo Zhang,Jinfei Liu,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结构感知的 TreeAgent，利用输入层级结构引导分段与记忆访问，在长文档处理中平均提升 3.61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆智能体将超长输入切成扁平 token 分段，不保留结构或层级，对 API 响应、HTML 等结构化数据造成三类问题：定长分段会拆散同一记录的字段关系；分段缺少层级信息导致结构上下文与位置模糊；连续的记忆更新可能丢失早期兄弟记录的细节，影响后续推理。

Method: TreeAgent 以输入层级结构驱动分段与历史信息访问：(1) 从完整子树及相邻兄弟子树组构造分段，保留局部结构关系；(2) 给出分段数量的理论保证，用于指导 token 预算选择，使其接近定长分段的分段数；(3) 为分段附加结构路径，使智能体可识别祖先与层级位置；(4) 引入从低层子节点分段指向高层父节点分段的残差记忆链接，形成超越连续记忆更新的直接信息流。

Result: 在 4 种代表性记忆智能体、8 个检索与聚合任务上评估，输入取自 JSON、HTML、YAML、JavaScript 的真实应用数据，长度 8K 至 1M token。四个数据集上四类智能体平均得分均有提升，平均提升 3.61%。

Conclusion: 保留输入的结构与层级信息可有效提升记忆智能体处理超长结构化输入的能力，且方法对不同记忆智能体具有通用性；理论分段数保证使结构分段与定长分段在 token 预算上可比，残差链接缓解了顺序记忆更新的信息丢失问题。

Abstract: Practical Web applications increasingly require agents to interpret long conversations or documents. For inputs exceeding the context windows of large language models (LLMs), memory agents process segments sequentially and retain past information in bounded memory. However, they typically divide inputs into flat token segments without preserving structure or hierarchy, causing three limitations for structured data such as API responses or HTML documents. First, fixed-length segmentation can split fields of the same record, breaking their relationships. Second, segments lack hierarchical information, obscuring their structural context and positions. Third, successive memory updates may discard details of earlier sibling records needed for reasoning on later segments. To address these limitations, we propose TreeAgent, a structure-aware agent that uses the input hierarchy to guide both segment-wise context processing and access to past information. Specifically, TreeAgent constructs segments from complete subtrees and groups of adjacent sibling subtrees to preserve local structural relationships. We provide theoretical guarantees on segment counts to guide token budget selection toward matching the segment count of fixed-length segmentation. We further attach structural paths to the segments for the agent to identify their ancestors and hierarchical positions. We also introduce residual memory links from the segments of low-level children to the segments of high-level parents, enabling direct information flows beyond successive memory updates. We evaluate TreeAgent with four representative memory agents on eight retrieval and aggregation tasks. The inputs are derived from real-world application data in JSON, HTML, YAML, and JavaScript and range from 8K to 1M tokens. TreeAgent improves the average score across the four datasets for all four agents, with 3.61% performance gain on average.

</details>


### [81] [Understanding Mixture-of-Experts Language Models Beyond Expert Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13975)
*Zhenhong Zhou,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Qiankun Li,Kun Wang,Junfeng Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Routing-Aware Magnitude Profiling (RAMP)从神经元粒度刻画MoE模型的领域特化组织,发现神经元具有稳定的领域专属性,且其重要性在预训练中持续重组而在后训练中保持稳定;据此提出RAMPr剪枝方法,在75%神经元保留率下保持91-98%原始性能,50%保留率下可降低36-43%显存并提升17-39%预填充吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE中专家特化的本质存在争议:一方报告领域相关的专家偏好,另一方则将路由主要归因于token身份或句法模式。作者认为仅停留在专家层级的路由分析不足以厘清分歧,需下沉到神经元层级考察领域特化计算的组织方式。

Method: 提出Routing-Aware Magnitude Profiling(RAMP)度量,结合路由信息与神经元幅值刻画神经元重要性;使用混合领域校准数据识别领域特化神经元集合,并考察其跨样本重叠度;在三个全开放MoE系列上追踪神经元重要性在预训练与后训练各checkpoint间的演化;基于RAMP提出RAMPr跨专家神经元剪枝,以实现多领域能力均衡保留。

Result: 同领域多源样本间领域特化神经元集合重叠度为58-74%,跨领域仅15-24%;预训练各checkpoint间神经元重要性持续重组,而专家级重要性排名相对稳定;后训练基本保持神经元排名。RAMPr在75%保留率下六模型平均保持91-98%原性能;50%保留率下降低36-43%常驻GPU显存,批处理预填充吞吐提升17-39%。

Conclusion: 领域特化在MoE中体现为神经元层级的结构化组织,而非仅由专家级路由或token身份决定;神经元重要性的动态演化规律为MoE训练分析与高效压缩提供了新视角,并验证了神经元级剪枝在多领域能力保持上的有效性。

Abstract: In Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, the nature of expert specialization remains controversial. Reports of domain-associated expert preferences contrast with analyses attributing routing primarily to token identity or syntactic patterns. To clarify these differing accounts, we look beyond expert-level routing to investigate the neuron-level organization of domain-specialized computation using Routing-Aware Magnitude Profiling (RAMP), a measure developed for this analysis. Using mixed-domain calibration data, we identify domain-specialized neuron sets with 58-74% overlap across multi-source samples of the same domain, compared with 15-24% across domains. To understand how this organization evolves during training, we trace neuron importance throughout pre-training and post-training across three fully open MoE families. Neuron importance continues to reorganize between pre-training checkpoints despite comparatively stable expert-level importance rankings, while post-training largely preserves neuron rankings. Building on these neuron-level perspectives, we further introduce RAMP-based Pruning (RAMPr) to select neurons across experts for balanced multi-domain capability preservation. At 75% neuron retention, RAMPr preserves average 91-98% of original full performance across six models. At 50% retention, our pruning method can reduce resident GPU memory by 36-43% and improves batched prefill throughput by 17-39% effectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/ydyjya/Neuron-Level-Specialization-in-MoE

</details>


### [82] [Personalized Memory Rubrics: Learning What to Remember per User via Rubric-Guided Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14017)
*Yupeng Han,Kai Zhang,Shuochen Liu,Ze Liu,Xianquan Wang,Zhihong Pan,Xiangyu Zhao,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Personalized Memory Rubrics (PMR) 框架，将终端问答反馈转化为逐步、逐用户的细粒度信用分配，实现个性化记忆更新策略与评分标准的闭环协同演化，且部署时零推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程个性化智能体的记忆更新策略学习困难：1）传统训练仅依赖终端 QA 准确率，反馈信号粗糙，对所有中间更新一视同仁；2）记忆动作的效用具有用户特异性，相同动作对不同用户画像可能产生相反效果，而现有方法采用全局共享评价标准与至多单一标量中间奖励，无法刻画这种异质性。

Method: PMR 将评估分解为通用层（捕捉跨画像的共性失败模式）与逐用户自适应层（以相对通用基线的专属增量建模个体特质）；通过 Rubric-Guided Credit Assignment 在 token 级别重分配信用，对成功轨迹中已验证的决策进行选择性放大，对失败轨迹回退到标准策略优化以避免不可靠归因；评分标准与记忆策略在带防循环保证的闭环中共同演化；部署时不依赖 rubric 或外部评判器。

Result: 在四个基准上相对强记忆增强基线取得一致提升，且推理阶段无额外开销。

Conclusion: 细粒度、个性化的过程级信用分配可有效改进长程智能体的记忆更新策略学习，验证了通用+用户增量式 rubric 分解与选择性信用重分配设计的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon personalized agents maintain an evolving memory by continuously deciding what to add, modify, or discard. However, learning an optimal update policy remains challenging. Conventional training relies solely on terminal question-answering accuracy, yielding a coarse feedback signal that treats all intermediate updates identically. Furthermore, update utility is inherently user-specific, as identical memory actions may yield opposite effects across different user personas. Existing methods fail to address this heterogeneity, relying on globally shared evaluation standards with at most a single scalar intermediate reward. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{Personalized Memory Rubrics} (\textbf{PMR}), a framework that translates terminal feedback into fine-grained, per-step, and per-user credit assignment. PMR decomposes evaluation into a universal layer capturing cross-persona failure patterns and a per-user adaptive layer modeling individual traits as a specialized delta over the universal baseline. Through \textbf{Rubric-Guided Credit Assignment}, PMR redistributes credit at the token level, selectively amplifying validated decisions on successful trajectories while falling back to standard policy optimization on failed ones to avoid unreliable attribution. The rubric and memory policy \textbf{co-evolve} in a closed loop with anti-circularity guarantees. Crucially, PMR incurs zero inference overhead, operating without any rubric or external judge during deployment. Extensive experiments across four benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over strong memory-augmented baselines.

</details>


### [83] [Hx2Dx: From Anamnesis to Diagnosis - A Multimodal Arena for Conversational Clinical Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14122)
*Federico Ravenda,Mirko Cesarini,Daniele Montagnani,Volodymyr Karpenko,Antonietta Mira,Andrea Raballo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出交互式诊断基准 Hx2Dx ARENA（基于 NEJM 的 1000 例病例），模拟从病史采集、体格检查到检查检验的完整诊疗过程，评估 LLM 的迭代问诊能力。结果显示所有模型在交互场景下诊断准确率均低于静态设置，差距主要源于提问不够精准、信息采集不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 诊断评测使用已整理好的病例摘要，只衡量"给定证据后的推理"，忽略了临床实际中获取证据的问诊过程，因此无法反映模型假设驱动的信息采集能力。

Method: 构建 Hx2Dx ARENA 交互式基准：选取 NEJM 的 1000 个临床病例，每次为模拟会诊，流程覆盖初诊表现→病史采集→体格检查→诊断性检查；在多种配置下评测开源与闭源 LLM，并与展示完整病例的静态设置对照。

Result: 交互设置下各模型表现差异显著；相比静态设置，所有模型的诊断准确率均下降；问题越具针对性、能构建更完整临床图景，诊断表现越好，提问能力可解释模型间差距与准确率损失的大部分。

Conclusion: LLM 在信息齐备时推理能力良好，但在假设驱动的信息收集中仍受限，即"不知该问什么才能知道"。弥补这一差距是其安全融入真实诊断流程的前提。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve expert-level diagnostic accuracy in static evaluations, where the model receives a curated vignette or case report that already condenses the diagnostically relevant findings. Such evaluations measure inference over already selected evidence; the evaluations do not account for the inquiry used to obtain it. In clinical practice, however, diagnosis is an iterative process of inquiry. Clinicians take the patient's history, or anamnesis (Hx), through targeted questioning, examine the patient, and request and interpret tests, progressively narrowing the differential until they reach a final diagnosis (Dx). We introduce Hx2Dx ARENA, an interactive benchmark of 1'000 clinical cases from the New England Journal of Medicine (NEJM). Each case is a simulated encounter that proceeds from initial presentation through history taking and physical examination to diagnostic workup. We evaluate open- and closed-source LLMs under multiple configurations. Performance varies considerably across models in the interactive setting, and every model loses diagnostic accuracy relative to the static setting, where the full case is presented upfront. The ability to ask the right questions accounts for much of both the gap between models and the loss of accuracy: diagnosis improves as questions become more targeted and build a more complete clinical picture. LLMs thus reason well once the relevant data are available but remain limited in hypothesis-driven information gathering, in knowing what to ask in order to know. Closing this gap is a prerequisite for their safe integration into real-world diagnostic workflows.

</details>


### [84] [BARS: Budget-Aware Sequential Sampling with Refusal for Majority Identification under Unknown Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14171)
*Shenxi Liu,Yiwei Li,Mingyang Zhao,Yuhang Tian,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BARS：将多数投票自洽性形式化为"未知分布+预算+目标错误率"下的多数识别问题，通过序贯规则验证top-two gap，实现提前停止、预算化多数投票或拒绝，并提供带证明的错误界与经验校准两种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复采样+多数选择的自我一致性虽有效，但现有提前终止变体在输出分布有利时虽能省成本，却无法从答案分布中给出正确的置信度估计，难以判断答案可靠性与预算是否充足。

Method: 把问题形式化为未知分布下带预算与目标错误率的多数识别；提出BARS序贯规则：认证top-two概率差（gap）后返回提前停止、预算化多数投票，或在可行性无法建立时给出拒绝。认证模式给出联合回答错误率与"拒绝本可处理实例"概率的严格界；实用模式用经验校准替代理论界，并在合成数据与GSM8K/MATH上评测。

Result: 合成实验中（数千随机种子、已知分布）复现了由难度驱动的行为，并测定了认证模式相对实用模式的代价。GSM8K上实用模式在含拒绝问题在内的同一池中比固定8次采样少抽22.3%样本，代价是拒绝18.8%的问题；MATH上输出答案准确率提升至82.5%，代价是诚实拒答约一半模型自身无法解决的难题。

Conclusion: 拒绝是对规则与预算的陈述而非"问题不可答"的判断，可指导实践者何时扩大预算或更换模型；BARS在节省采样成本的同时提供可靠置信度，并以可控拒答率换取更高输出准确率。

Abstract: Repeated sampling with majority selection is an effective means of enforcing self-consistency in large language models. Recently, a variant has also been introduced that terminates early to save costs when the output distribution is favorable. But it still cannot yield a correct confidence estimate from the answer distribution. For this issue, we formalize this as majority identification under an unknown distribution with a budget and a target error rate, and propose BARS, a sequential rule certifying the top-two gap and returning an early stop, a budgeted majority vote, or a refusal when feasibility cannot be established. A refusal is a statement about the rule and its budget, not a claim that a question is unanswerable, and it tells practitioners when to widen the budget or switch models. A certified mode carries proven bounds on the joint answering error and on refusing an instance the budget could handle, while a practical mode, used in our experiments, replaces them with empirical calibration. On synthetic instances drawn from known distributions over thousands of seeds, the difficulty-driven behavior is recovered and the price of the certified mode is measured.On GSM8K the practical mode draws 22.3\% fewer samples than fixed-8 sampling over the same pool, refused questions included, at the cost of declining 18.8\% of the questions. On MATH, it greatly improves the accuracy of its output answers into 82.5\% at the cost of honestly refusing to answer half of the difficult questions that the model itself is unable to solve.

</details>


### [85] [Cross-Border Fulfillment Center Selection via Doubly Robust Estimation and Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14174)
*Kriti Mahajan,Patrick Bloebaum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出跨境履约中心（XB FC）选择模型——融合预测、双重稳健估计与线性规划的多阶段优化框架，在三条生产级国际航线上较人工策略显著降低时效与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨境电商需在固定国内网络下，从源国多个履约中心中为每条"源—目的"航运弧选择发货源，需同时兼顾配送时效、成本、利润与发货量，属于多目标、多阶段决策问题，而现有人工专家规则难以实现最优权衡。

Method: 构建多阶段优化框架：先做需求/时效预测，再用双重稳健（doubly robust）估计评估各候选源FC的因果效应，最后以线性规划求解在时效、成本、利润、发货量多目标下的源FC子集选择；并以某大型电商12个月发货数据在三条生产级国际航运走廊上评估，采用聚合离线回放与发货级反事实最近邻匹配两种验证方式。

Result: 相对人工专家定义的FC选择策略，三条走廊上配送时间降低8.5%–51.1%，物流成本降低4.4%–48.3%，5/5目标达标率（5天、5美元）最高提升6.5个百分点；Pareto分析显示模型在效率—再分配前沿上稳定优于单目标排序策略与无约束LP，结果均具统计显著性。

Conclusion: 该多阶段优化+因果估计框架可在真实生产规模下有效改进跨境履约的源FC选择，在时效、成本与合规率上取得一致的帕累托改进，具备工业落地价值。

Abstract: We introduce the cross-border (XB) fulfillment center (FC) Selection Model: a multi-stage optimization framework combining forecasting, doubly robust estimation, and linear programming to select optimal source FCs. For each source-destination shipping arc, the objective is to choose a subset of source-country FCs that minimize delivery time and cost while maximizing profit and shipped units under a fixed domestic network. We evaluate the model on three production-scale international shipping corridors for a large e-commerce company using 12 months of shipment data, employing both aggregate offline replay and shipment-level counterfactual nearest-neighbor matching. Across all shipping corridors, the XB FC Selection Model achieves 8.5-51.1% reductions in delivery time and 4.4-48.3% reductions in logistics cost relative to human expert-defined FC selection policies, with up to 6.5 percentage-point improvements in 5/5 target compliance (5 days, $5 USD). Pareto analysis shows the model consistently dominates single-objective ranking strategies and unconstrained LP on the efficiency-reallocation frontier, with all results statistically significant.

</details>


### [86] [An Augmented Lagrangian Approach to Lifted Bellman Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14183)
*Jongchan Park,Hyukjun Yang,Narim Jeong,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用乘子法求解离线RL的Lifted Bellman线性规划（LBLP），将约束系数作为对偶乘子学习，得到无目标网络、单Q/V的AL-LBLP，在50个OGBench任务上平均成功率60.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TD评论家需回归自举目标并通常依赖目标网络；LBLP将离线RL的样本内最优值表述为数据集上的线性规划，但其神经求解器ALBUM以固定系数的铰链惩罚松弛约束，理论保证仅是对预先选定系数的条件性结论，缺乏原则性。

Method: 采用乘子法（method of multipliers）求解同一线性规划：惩罚系数由对偶上升学习为乘子，仅保留一个惩罚参数ρ。凸情形下不动点即LBLP的KKT对，任意驻点处约束权重与目标精确平衡。评论家可支持高斯actor、flow actor与best-of-N采样；针对高斯actor提出优势归一化的DDPG+BC目标。

Result: 单Q、单V、无目标网络的AL-LBLP配合best-of-N在50个state-based OGBench任务上平均成功率60.1%；长时序任务上humanoidmaze-giant达81.5%、antmaze-giant达79.8%；batch size扩大至8192仍未崩溃。

Conclusion: 以对偶上升自适应学习约束乘子替代固定惩罚系数，为LBLP提供了具KKT驻点保证的求解方式，并实现无目标网络、可扩展至大batch的稳定离线RL评论家。

Abstract: Temporal-difference critics regress onto a bootstrapped target and usually need a target network. Lifted Bellman Linear Programming (LBLP) instead expresses the in-sample optimal values of offline RL as the solution to a linear program over the dataset. Its neural solver ALBUM relaxes the constraints into hinge penalties with fixed coefficients, and its guarantees are conditions on coefficients chosen in advance. We solve the same program with the method of multipliers: the coefficients become multipliers learned by dual ascent, with one penalty parameter $ρ$. In the convex case the fixed points are the KKT pairs of LBLP, and at every stationary point the applied constraint weights balance the objective exactly. The critic can serve a Gaussian actor, a flow actor, and best-of-N sampling, and for the Gaussian actor we propose an advantage-normalized DDPG+BC objective. With a single $Q$ and $V$ and no target network, our AL-LBLP with best-of-N reaches 60.1\% average success on the 50 state-based OGBench tasks. On long-horizon tasks, it reaches 81.5% on humanoidmaze-giant and 79.8% on antmaze-giant. It does not collapse up to batch size 8192.

</details>


### [87] [Joint Quantization and Dynamic Partitioning for Context-Aware Edge Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14196)
*Zhu Jiaqi,Jain Prakriti,Katare Dewant,Kumar Saurabh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向车联网（CAV）的边端协同编排框架，联合优化ViT模型的分割点、逐层量化位宽与执行放置，以MILP最小化计算与V2V通信能耗，在满足端到端时延与任务质量约束下显著降低通信能耗与完成时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联网自动驾驶车辆上部署视觉Transformer需协同管理计算与V2V通信。现有静态分割推理采用固定切分点，无法适应时变链路带宽、处理负载与多流并发流量，导致能效与时延表现不佳。

Method: 将模型分割点选择、逐层混合精度量化位宽分配与异构车载计算节点上的执行放置联合建模为混合整数线性规划（MILP），目标为最小化计算与通信能耗，约束为端到端时延与任务质量。在Jetson Orin Nano(8GB)、Orin NX(16GB)、AGX Orin(32GB)上，使用OPV2V驱动的多流工作负载（K∈{2,4,6,8}）进行评测。

Result: 相对Uniform INT8分割基线，V2V通信能耗降低33.3%–36.0%；K=8时端到端任务完成时延在Orin Nano上降低34.0%、Orin NX上降低33.1%；相对FP16执行，3D检测精度（mAP50）仅下降3.1–4.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 联合分割与混合精度编排能有效适配动态车载网络与多流负载，在精度损失可控的前提下同时取得显著通信节能与时延收益，优于固定分割的静态方案。

Abstract: Deploying vision transformer models across connected and automated vehicles (CAVs) requires joint management of computation and Vehicle-to-Vehicle (V2V) communication. Static split-inference methods use fixed partition points and cannot adapt to time-varying link bandwidth, processing load, and concurrent multi-stream traffic. This paper proposes an edge orchestration framework that jointly selects model partition points, per-layer quantization bitwidths, and execution placement across heterogeneous vehicular compute nodes. The joint partitioning and mixed-precision allocation problem is formulated as a Mixed-Integer Linear Program (MILP) that minimizes compute and communication energy subject to end-to-end latency and task-quality constraints. The framework is evaluated on Jetson Orin Nano (8~GB), Jetson Orin NX (16~GB), and Jetson AGX Orin (32~GB) nodes using OPV2V-driven multi-stream workloads with $K \in \{2,4,6,8\}$. Across the evaluated configurations, the proposed formulation reduces V2V communication energy by $33.3$--$36.0\%$ relative to a Uniform INT8 split. At $K=8$, end-to-end task completion latency is reduced by $34.0\%$ on the Orin Nano and $33.1\%$ on the Orin NX relative to the same baseline, while limiting the reduction in 3D detection accuracy to $3.1$--$4.1$ percentage points in $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ relative to FP16 execution.

</details>


### [88] [Breakdown of Autoregressive Multi-Step Reasoning Under Concentrated vs. Diffuse Attention Head Dropout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14209)
*Karol Struniawski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在GSM8K上对两个8B模型配对评测250组推导：将注意力头掩码以"局部爆发"方式施加，相比同等预算的弥散i.i.d.丢弃，会显著降低推理准确率（Qwen3-8B −12.4pp，LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct −7.2pp），说明自回归推理链对时空集中的结构性扰动尤为脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受清醒期延长时新皮层网络出现局部短暂"off-state"（神经元沉默）现象启发，探究类似的时空集中型扰动是否同样影响自回归LLM的顺序推理能力，并为Transformer架构的容错性提供经验基准。

Method: 在GSM8K上构建250组配对推导，评测Qwen3-8B与LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct；将"集中爆发式注意力头掩码"与"预算匹配的弥散i.i.d. dropout"对比；并用分层五分位匹配把相同累计预算均匀分散到各生成步；另做探索性因子分离实验（N=100）考察单步丢弃密度与多token连续性，统计采用精确McNemar检验与Holm校正。

Result: 起始参数B=0.10（约覆盖5.7%脆弱头-token体量）时，Qwen3-8B从弥散噪声下91.2%降至78.8%（Δ=−12.4pp，p=5.5×10⁻⁶）；LLaMA从84.0%降至76.8%（Δ=−7.2pp，p=0.0198）。将同等累计预算均匀分散后退化明显更轻（Qwen 91.2%、LLaMA 84.0%维持不变）。因子实验中单步丢弃密度与准确率急性下降相关（Qwen −10.0pp，p_holm=0.0425），多token连续性呈不显著复合趋势（−7.0pp，p_holm=0.1892）。误差分析：Qwen失败多为格式完整但推导出错（条件准确率下降−11.4pp）；LLaMA中22.2%的失配样本缺失最终答案格式行（条件差距缩至−5.0pp）。

Conclusion: 自回归推理链对集中的结构性扰动高度脆弱，扰动的空间集中度与时间分布方式比总扰动预算更关键；该结果为Transformer故障容错研究提供了可复现的经验基准与评测范式。

Abstract: During extended wakefulness, mammalian neocortical networks experience localized periods of transient neuronal silence termed off-states. Here, we examine how analogous spatiotemporally concentrated disruptions affect sequential reasoning in autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs). Evaluating 250 paired derivations on the GSM8K benchmark across two 8B models (Qwen3-8B and LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct), we contrast clustered bursts of attention head masking against budget-matched diffuse i.i.d. dropout. Concentrated bursts induce substantial reasoning degradation: at onset parameter $\mathcal{B}=0.10$ (scheduling $\sim 5.7\%$ of vulnerable head-token volume), Qwen3-8B accuracy drops from $91.2\%$ under diffuse noise to $78.8\%$ under burst dropout ($Δ= -12.4$ percentage points [pp], exact McNemar $p = 5.5 \times 10^{-6}$), while LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct drops from $84.0\%$ to $76.8\%$ ($Δ= -7.2$ pp, $p = 0.0198$). Conversely, dispersing the identical cumulative budget uniformly across generation steps via stratified quintile matching yields substantially milder degradation (retaining $91.2\%$ in Qwen and $84.0\%$ in LLaMA). In an exploratory factorial dissociation ($N=100$), single-step dropout density is associated with an acute drop in a single schedule draw ($-10.0$ pp on Qwen, $p_{\text{holm}} = 0.0425$), while multi-token continuity exhibits an inconclusive compounding trend ($-7.0$ pp, $p_{\text{holm}} = 0.1892$). Error analysis reveals that Qwen failures reflect derivation errors within formatted responses (conditional accuracy drop of $-11.4$ pp), whereas in LLaMA $22.2\%$ of discordant losses lack a final answer format line (conditional gap narrows to $-5.0$ pp). These findings demonstrate that autoregressive reasoning chains are particularly vulnerable to concentrated structural disruptions, providing empirical benchmarks for fault tolerance in transformer architectures.

</details>


### [89] [Personalized Affective Perception via Continual Learning in Human-Agent Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14247)
*Mehmet Onur Keskin,Berk Buzcu,Nikhil Churamani,Reyhan Aydogan,Hatice Gunes,Catholijn Jonker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结合持续学习与表情想象（CLIFER）的情感自适应谈判策略，利用差分反馈将情绪变化映射为效用目标调整，实现对个体面部情感感知的实时个性化。66人被试内实验显示，相比FaceChannel基线，该策略显著提升智能体个体效用与人类让步率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自利型智能体与人类谈判时需解读非言语表情并调整行为，但通用面部情感模型在个体与情境间存在泛化差距（generalization gap），难以个性化感知效价-唤醒度。

Method: 在CLIFER（结合持续学习与面部表情识别想象）基础上构建情感自适应谈判策略：用差分反馈将效价-唤醒度的方向与强度变化转化为智能体目标效用的调整；采用异步架构在实时交互过程中后台更新CLIFER；开展66名被试、与人形机器人谈判的平衡被试内实验，与FaceChannel基线对比。

Result: 持续学习条件下智能体获得显著更高的个体效用，并引发显著更高的被试让步率；唤醒度估计均值显著更高且输出范围更宽；被试效用与Nash距离在两种条件间无显著差异。

Conclusion: 将持续性情感个性化与差分反馈相结合，对实时谈判具有实际价值；但效用分配均衡性与谈判效率（Nash距离）未受损害，需在保持公平性的前提下进一步验证个性化机制的可推广性。

Abstract: Self-interested intelligent agents that negotiate with humans should interpret nonverbal expressions and adapt their behavior accordingly. However, generic facial-affect models degrade across individuals and contexts, creating a generalization gap. We introduce an affect-adaptive negotiation strategy that incorporates Continual Learning with Imagination for Facial Expression Recognition (CLIFER) to personalize valence-arousal perception in real time. Differential feedback converts changes in valence-arousal direction and intensity into adjustments of the agent's target utility. An asynchronous architecture updates CLIFER in the background during live interaction. In a counterbalanced within-subject study in which 66 participants negotiated with a humanoid robot, the CL-enabled agent achieved significantly higher individual utility and elicited higher participant concession rates than the FaceChannel baseline. The CL condition yielded significantly higher mean arousal estimates, alongside a broader output range. Participant utility and Nash distance did not change significantly between conditions. These outcomes support the practical value of coupling continual affective personalization with differential feedback for real-time negotiation.

</details>


### [90] [A Cross-Scenario Survey and Analysis of Self-Evolving Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14256)
*Jingjing Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述研究多模态自进化智能体跨场景迁移的结构性不对称来源，以三阶段自进化循环为分析框架，将各机制重述为"以任务结构资源换取稀缺要素"的权衡，并判定其跨场景可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有自进化机制多在单一场景内设计与评估，其前置条件在跨场景迁移下尚未验证，导致机制迁移呈现不对称现象且缺乏结构性解释。

Method: 1）以四条准则与"自指"判别标准形式化自进化多模态智能体；2）采用"经验生成—反馈获取—更新固化"三阶段循环作为分析框架，沿 rollout 次数上限与反馈保真度两个维度定位四个场景（多模态推理、多模态生成、GUI 与 Web 导航、具身操作）；3）在各场景内对比自进化循环与固定循环，区分场景固有局限与自进化设计内在局限；4）将每个机制重述为"以输出等价性、可校验结果等任务结构资源为前提提升稀缺要素"的权衡，并跨场景判定为可移植、有条件、受阻或冗余。

Result: 将迁移不对称归因于两个相反迁移方向上稀缺要素与任务结构资源组合的差异，并指出可采用匹配预算的测量方式来检验这些尚未验证的判定结论。

Conclusion: 自进化机制的跨场景可迁移性取决于稀缺要素与任务结构资源的匹配关系而非机制本身优劣；现有迁移判定仍待匹配预算实验验证。

Abstract: Recent work shows that multimodal agents increasingly self-improve without recurring human annotation. However, each self-improvement mechanism is designed and evaluated within a single scenario, leaving its preconditions unverified under cross-scenario transfer. This survey investigates the structural source of asymmetric mechanism transfer across four scenarios: multimodal reasoning, multimodal generation, GUI and Web navigation, and embodied manipulation. We first formalize the self-evolving multimodal agent by four criteria and a differentia of self-reference, and adopt the three-stage self-evolving loop of experience generation, feedback acquisition, and update consolidation as our analytical framework to position the scenarios along ceilings on rollout count and feedback fidelity. Comparing self-evolving with fixed loops within each scenario separates the limitations that the scenario imposes from those inherent in the self-evolving design. We then recast each mechanism as a trade-off that raises a scarce factor by presupposing a task-structural resource, such as output equivalence or checkable outcomes, and judge it portable, conditional, blocked, or redundant across scenarios. We attribute the asymmetry to divergent combinations of scarce factors and resources across opposite transfer directions, and identify the matched-budget measurements that could overturn these still untested verdicts.

</details>


### [91] [Recurrent Modular Structure in Spiking Neural Network Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14262)
*Vikram Ramavarapu,Himanshu Udupi,Ashish Gautam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出社区循环脉冲神经网络（CoRe SNN），将循环网络划分为固定角色的社区，脉冲活动表现为社区间随机游走，动作由游走到达的社区决定，全部学习为局部三因子规则，并通过动态模块化（Map-STDP）、部分模块化（SBM初始化）与完全模块化（封sealing）三种方式获得社区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经回路兼具模块化与循环性（稠密社区间稀疏连接），而强化学习用SNN多为前馈结构，尚不清楚如何在循环SNN中获得功能模块及其收益。

Method: 构建CoRe SNN：控制器社区接收刺激、每动作一个社区、隐社区承载内部状态；脉冲活动为社区间游走，动作为到达社区。学习全部为局部三因子规则。比较三种模块化获得机制：动态模块化（新规则Map-STDP在学习中促进模块结构）、部分模块化（SBM初始化部分强制）、完全模块化（sealing完全强制）。

Result: 在CartPole上、三种脉冲神经元模型中，Map-STDP优于无该规则的同一网络，在其中两种模型上匹配完全模块化（sealed），并使脉冲游走更具可压缩性。用循环动作社区替换TD-STDP actor-critic的前馈actor，在CartPole与LunarLander上改进、在Acrobot上持平。模块化强度依赖任务：完全模块化在Acrobot最有益，LunarLander上三种机制表现相近。

Conclusion: 循环模块化网络可匹配甚至超越前馈方法，并提升生物合理性与能效。

Abstract: Biological neural circuits are both modular and recurrent, with dense communities sparsely linked to one another. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) for reinforcement learning are usually feed-forward, and it is unclear how to obtain functional modules in a recurrent SNN or what they buy. In this work, we introduce a class of spiking networks, called Community Recurrent SNNs (CoRe SNNs): recurrent networks partitioned into communities with fixed roles. A controller community receives the stimulus, each action has its own community, and latent communities carry internal state. Spiking activity is then a walk between communities, and the action is the community the walk reaches. All learning is local and three-factor, and the recurrent communities are closer to cortical microcircuits than a feed-forward actor. We compare three regimes for obtaining the communities: dynamic modularity, in which a new learning rule we introduce, Map-STDP, encourages modular structure during learning; partial modularity, in which a stochastic block model (SBM) initialization enforces it partly; and full modularity, in which sealing enforces it fully. On CartPole, across three spiking neuron models, Map-STDP improves learning over the same network without it matches full modularity (sealed communities) on two of the three models, and makes the observed spike walk more compressible. Replacing the feed-forward actor of a TD-STDP actor-critic with recurrent action communities improves on the feed-forward network on CartPole and LunarLander, and matches it on Acrobot. How strongly to enforce modularity depends on the task: full modularity helps most on Acrobot, while on LunarLander the dynamic, partial and full regimes perform alike. Overall, these recurrent modular networks can match or even improve upon feed-forward approaches, with improvements in bio-plausibility and energy efficiency.

</details>


### [92] [LM-SimBench: A Unified Framework, Dataset, and Benchmark for Large Language Model Training Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14285)
*Yu Dong,Hong Li,Qiang Sun,Xu Zhou,Jinbiao Huo,Qixiu Cheng,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LM-SimBench——一个统一框架、性能画像数据集与基准，用于系统地评估 LLM 分布式训练模拟器，覆盖从模型编译到调度级聚合的功能阶段，并在 Ascend NPU 集群上采集 10 个稠密/MoE 模型家族、23 种模型设置、348 个可行配置，支持跨平台比较与配置选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过真实分布式训练跑次来评估候选并行策略，在大规模下成本高到不可行；性能模拟虽更廉价，但现有模拟器所建模的训练阶段与被评估工作负载不同，难以进行受控比较，缺乏统一评估基准。

Method: 1) 提出 LM-SimBench 统一框架，将模拟分解为从模型编译到调度级聚合的功能阶段，把执行表示与代价估计器连接到可度量的评估目标；2) 构建性能画像数据集：在 Ascend NPU 集群上对 10 个稠密与 MoE LLM 家族的 23 种模型设置进行剖析，得到 348 个可行配置，联合变化 5 个并行维度与激活重计算，提供配置、rank、算子、事件、kernel 六种粒度视图；3) 对代表性训练模拟器进行评估。

Result: 评估表明 LM-SimBench 支持预测精度比较、配置选择评估以及预测误差诊断三类任务；匹配的 GPU-NPU 实验验证了该数据集与评估协议的跨平台适用性，并展示了由 NPU 引导的 GPU 配置选择。

Conclusion: LM-SimBench 为 LLM 训练模拟器提供了统一、可复现的评测基准，弥补了现有模拟器在建模阶段与工作负载上的不可比问题，并可跨硬件平台迁移指导并行配置选择。

Abstract: Evaluating candidate parallelization strategies through distributed training runs makes strategy selection prohibitively expensive at scale. Performance simulation offers a lower cost alternative, yet controlled comparisons among existing simulators remain difficult because they differ in the training stages they model and the workloads on which they are evaluated. We introduce LM-SimBench, a unified framework, profiling dataset, and benchmark for evaluating LLM training simulators. The framework decomposes simulation into functional stages from model compilation to schedule level aggregation, connecting execution representations and cost estimators to measurable evaluation targets. Following this framework, we profile 23 model settings from 10 dense and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLM families on an Ascend Neural Processing Unit (NPU) cluster. The resulting dataset contains 348 feasible configurations that jointly vary five parallelism dimensions and activation recomputation, with six views spanning configuration, rank, operator, event, and kernel granularity. Evaluations of representative training simulators demonstrate how LM-SimBench supports comparisons of prediction accuracy, assessment of configuration selection, and diagnosis of prediction errors. Matched GPU-NPU experiments validate the cross-platform applicability of the collection and evaluation protocol and demonstrate NPU-guided GPU configuration selection.

</details>


### [93] [Multimodal Abstention That Preserves Acquired Correct Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14311)
*Jinyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ReFrain，将"弃答修复"建模为在"新丢失正确答案预算"约束下最大化不可答问题的正确弃答率，在 Qwen2.5-VL-3B 上实现零预算零损失，并在 0.5% 预算下显著优于 CLIP-UP EmbLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为任务适配后的多模态模型加入弃答能力会误伤原本正确的回答，并可能抹去适配阶段获得的能力；现有弃答方法缺乏对"丢失正确答案"这一代价的显式约束与度量。

Method: 将弃答修复形式化为带预算约束的优化问题（约束：新增丢失正确答案数 ≤ 预算；目标：最大化不可答问题正确弃答率）。ReFrain 由三部分组成：(1) 基于首 token logits 的线性探针；(2) 在留出图像簇上的阈值校准；(3) 确定性 veto 规则——将被拒绝的输出替换为已展示的"none of the above"选项，其余回答保持不变。所有对比方法均从同一 answering-SFT checkpoint 出发，分别追踪丢失正确答案、修复错误与保留的 SFT 增益。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-3B 上，零预算时 ReFrain 保留全部 57 个 SFT 增益答案且零损失，正确弃答率提升 7.30 个百分点；0.5% 校准预算下达到 74.05% 正确弃答、仅 8 例损失、保留 55 个增益；CLIP-UP EmbLoRA 为 64.65%，但损失 62、修复 41、保留增益 40。同一探针在默认阈值下损失 298 个正确答案。冻结模型与阈值后在 228 个独立问题簇上确认：0.5% 点处 66.05% 正确弃答、零损失，超 EmbLoRA 32.72 个百分点。常规拒绝式 SFT 正确弃答更高但回归代价更大。

Conclusion: 将弃答视为受"回归预算"约束的修复问题，可在几乎不损害已获得能力的前提下有效提升弃答正确率；轻量探针加簇级阈值校准与确定性 veto 是一种高性价比、可复现的方案，且优于需要额外适配的 EmbLoRA 式方法。

Abstract: Adding abstention to a task-adapted multimodal model can discard correct answers, including capabilities acquired during adaptation. We formulate abstention repair as maximizing correct abstention on unanswerable questions under a budget on newly lost correct answers. We propose ReFrain (Regression-Free Abstention), which combines a linear probe on first-token logits, threshold calibration on held-out image clusters, and a deterministic veto that replaces rejected outputs with the displayed "none of the above" option while preserving all other responses. "Regression-free" denotes zero loss on the calibration set at the zero-budget target. All abstention methods in our main comparison start from the same answering-SFT checkpoint; we separately track lost correct answers, repaired errors, and retained SFT gains. On Qwen2.5-VL-3B, ReFrain at zero budget retains all 57 SFT-gained answers with no losses and improves correct abstention by 7.30 percentage points. At a 0.5% calibration budget, it reaches 74.05% correct abstention with 8 losses and retains 55 gains; CLIP-UP EmbLoRA reaches 64.65% with 62 losses, 41 repairs, and 40 retained gains. The same probe at its default threshold loses 298 correct answers. With models and thresholds frozen, confirmation on 228 independent question clusters yields 66.05% correct abstention and no losses at the 0.5% point, exceeding EmbLoRA by 32.72 percentage points. Ordinary refusal SFT achieves higher correct abstention at greater regression cost. Code and reproduction materials: https://github.com/ModeLiberaL/ReFrain.

</details>


### [94] [Don't Just Trust Your Memory: Evidence-Linked Memory Reduces Memory-Induced Degradation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14320)
*Yukyeong Lee,Yumin Choi,Jinheon Baek,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WikiTrace 是一种"证据可溯"的外部记忆系统：将每条经验教训与"轨迹成败原因摘要"及原始轨迹链接，使智能体在行动前能逐层回溯核验或修正教训，从而缓解记忆导致的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体常用外部记忆将历史任务轨迹蒸馏为简洁"教训"以改进新任务表现，但会产生记忆诱发的性能退化（memory-induced degradation）：智能体在无记忆时能解出的任务却失败，原因常是遵循了错误教训，或误用、误读正确教训。根源在于教训仅由"成功/失败"标签的轨迹推断而来，使用时却脱离原始轨迹呈现，智能体无从核验。

Method: 提出 WikiTrace：每条教训以简洁洞见（insight）形式存储，并链接到该轨迹成败原因的摘要，再链接到原始轨迹本身。在新任务上，智能体从全部洞见出发；当某条洞见信息不足、与其他洞见冲突、或源自失败任务时，借助链接与终端工具逐层下探，补全缺失细节或纠正教训后再行动。

Result: 在五个智能体基准上验证，WikiTrace 一致优于现有记忆方法与"仅提供相同洞见"的基线，同时降低记忆诱发的性能退化。

Conclusion: 为教训建立"洞见—成败原因摘要—原始轨迹"的证据链，是缓解记忆诱发退化、提升记忆型智能体可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: External memory enables LLM agents to improve from experience by distilling trajectories of past tasks into concise lessons for new tasks. However, memory can also lead to memory-induced degradation, where an agent fails on tasks it would solve without memory, often because it follows a lesson that is incorrect, or misapplies or misreads a valid one. This is because lessons are inferred from trajectories labeled only with task success or failure, yet are typically provided without the trajectories they came from, leaving the agent no way to check them. To address this, we introduce WikiTrace, an evidence-linked memory system designed to let agents check each lesson against its original trajectory. Specifically, motivated by the finding that a trajectory often still holds the evidence needed to catch an erroneous lesson, WikiTrace stores each lesson as a concise insight linked to a summary of why its trajectory succeeded or failed and to the raw trajectory itself. Then, on a new task, the agent starts from all insights and, whenever one lacks details needed to act, conflicts with another, or comes from a failed task, descends through its links with terminal tools to fill in missing details or correct the insight before acting. We validate WikiTrace on five agentic benchmarks, showing that it consistently outperforms existing memory approaches and a baseline given the same insights alone, while also reducing memory-induced degradation. Our code is available at https://github.com/yukyeongleee/wikitrace

</details>


### [95] [When Should Agents Remember? Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14326)
*Runxuan Tang,Haoyu Gao,Yuyan Ding,Junyi Yao,Zihao Zheng,Zecheng Sheng,Liwei Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution（FGSE），将经验教训表述为带前置条件、行为变更、预期效果与验证器的结构化假设，先在临时状态中经目标迁移、证伪与归档回归探针检验，再据增益与风险决定提交、修正或拒绝，从而提升长程智能体自进化的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地运行在长程、验证器丰富的环境中，但现有自进化机制常把由单个episode得出的"教训"直接写入记忆。局部成功并不等于可复用、安全的知识，容易导致过拟合、证伪失败与回归损害。核心问题是：一条由经验导出的行为更新，何时才应被接纳进智能体的持久状态。

Method: 提出FGSE框架：(1) 将每个候选教训表示成结构化假设，显式包含precondition、behavior change、expected effect与verifier；(2) 仅在临时状态中应用该假设；(3) 在目标迁移探针、证伪探针和归档回归探针上测试；(4) 依据实测增益与风险评估，对假设执行commit / refine / reject三选一的门控操作。

Result: 在Web、App、工具使用与长期记忆基准上取得强任务表现：WebArena成功率39.8，AppWorld平均完成度78.8，τ-Retail/τ-Airline的Pass^1为0.724/0.487；同时将证伪失败降至5.0%、回归损害降至1.3%、有害已提交更新降至3.6%。

Conclusion: 将记忆更新当作假设来检验，而非直接固化为知识，可支撑更可靠的长期适应。该结果表明显式的证伪门控是长程智能体自进化中筛选可复用、安全行为更新的有效机制。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate in long-horizon, verifier-rich environments, but current self-improvement mechanisms often write episode-derived lessons directly into memory, making local success an unreliable signal for reusable and safe knowledge. This paper addresses the core question of when an experience-derived behavioral update should be admitted into persistent agent state. We propose Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution (FGSE), a verifier-grounded framework that represents each candidate lesson as a structured hypothesis with an explicit precondition, behavior change, expected effect, and verifier. FGSE applies the hypothesis only in a temporary state, tests it on target-transfer, falsification, and archived-regression probes, and then commits, refines, or rejects it according to measured gain and risk. Across web, app, tool-use, and long-term memory benchmarks, FGSE achieves strong task performance, including 39.8 WebArena success rate, 78.8 AppWorld average completion, and 0.724/0.487 Pass$^1$ on $τ$-Retail/$τ$-Airline, while reducing falsification failures to 5.0%, regression damage to 1.3%, and harmful committed updates to 3.6%. These results suggest that testing memory updates as hypotheses supports more reliable long-term adaptation.

</details>


### [96] [CLADE: Category-Level Answering from Deficient Evidence in Retrieval-Augmented Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14355)
*S M Rayeed,Charles V. Stewart,Yao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"可解析式不足"(resolvable insufficiency)这一新情境：检索片段精确相关但粒度偏粗，并揭示模型会直接作答而非弃答或继续检索的"Clade"现象，指出经本体映射可将粗粒度片段恢复为精确答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文充分性研究把粗粒度段落一律视为"不充分"，未区分"信息缺失"与"信息粒度偏粗但可由领域本体细化"这两类情况，从而忽略了模型在此情境下应弃答/检索、而非猜测的行为问题。

Method: 在PrimeKG生物医学问答上构造family-only（仅含上位疾病家族名）段落，与无信息段落对比测弃答率；用反事实测试（把精确疾病名替换为上位家族名 vs 删除整句）确认因果；用置信度过滤、更大模型、显式弃答指令等基线干预；在GloBI生态物种互作上复现；最后测试本体能否把粗段落转为精确答案（金标家族下本体列表召回、字符串匹配定位家族、模型自选家族）。

Result: family-only段落相比无信息段落使弃答率下降13.8个百分点，猜错率约50%；反事实测试中替换上位名相较删句使弃答率降17.4个百分点；置信度过滤、放大模型、弃答指令均无法消除Clade，且在GloBI上复现。给定正确家族时本体列表含真答案比例98.5%，但字符串匹配仅19.8%能在页面定位该家族；改用模型自选家族提升至30.0%，端到端恢复率从10.0%升至26.8%。

Conclusion: 粗粒度但相关的证据应被视为独立类别，模型在此情境下倾向"按类作答"而非弃答；简单干预无效，需引入本体进行细化，而"页面定位家族"与"家族选择"仍是端到端流程的未解瓶颈。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented systems often retrieve passages that are accurate and relevant but state the answer at a coarser level of granularity than the question requires. For example, a question may ask which specific disease a drug treats (familial hyperlipidemia), while the retrieved passage mentions the broader disease family (hyperlipidemia). Prior work on context sufficiency treats such coarse passages simply as insufficient. We argue that they form a distinct case, which we call resolvable insufficiency: text alone cannot settle the fine-grained answer, but the broader category it mentions maps to a short list of candidate answers in a domain ontology. When language models read these coarse, family-only passages, they exhibit a phenomenon we call Clade (Category-Level Answering from Deficient Evidence): they answer instead of abstaining or searching further. On biomedical questions from PrimeKG, family-only passages reduce abstention by 13.8 points compared to uninformative passages, and the resulting guesses are wrong about half the time. A counterfactual test confirms the cause: replacing an exact disease name with its broader family name lowers abstention by 17.4 points compared to deleting the sentence. Simple fixes like confidence filtering, scaling to a larger model, and explicitly instructing the model to abstain cannot resolve Clade, and this replicates on ecological species interactions in GloBI. Finally, we test whether an ontology can turn coarse passages into exact answers. Given the correct family, an ontology list includes the true answer in 98.5% of cases, but string matching finds that family on the page for only 19.8%. Letting the model choose the family raises this to 30.0% and end-to-end recovery from 10.0% to 26.8%. Overall, our results show when language models should stop guessing and consult an ontology, and highlight which steps of that process remain unsolved.

</details>


### [97] [Cognitive Mapping of Learning by Thinking Mechanisms in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14382)
*Dong Han,Jin Zhang,Kunyue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出认知式"思维学习"(LbT)框架，将反思推理、合成记忆生成、内部模拟、概念自组织四种机制映射到LLM的计算过程，指出相关方法可近似实现这些机制并提升部分任务表现，但其典型失败源于LLM与人类认知间的四种结构性不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益表现出类似人类"思维学习"（无需外部新输入、通过解释与推理等内部过程获取新见解）的能力，但对其背后认知机制及其局限尚缺乏系统性解释，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 构建认知LbT框架，将四种人类LbT机制（反思推理、合成记忆生成、内部模拟、概念自组织）对应到LLM的计算过程；结合代表性方法分析（chain-of-thought、self-consistency、self-reflection、Tree-of-Thoughts、Self-Instruct、self-taught reasoning），并归纳其失败模式及四种结构性不对称（生成器与监控器同一、无外部锚定的闭环学习、符号性而非感觉运动性模拟、推理期计算成本）。

Result: 所选方法可在功能上近似上述机制，并在无额外标注样本条件下提升部分任务表现，但不必然形成参数层面的持久学习；例如Self-Instruct仅用175个种子任务生成约52,000条指令，在Super-NaturalInstructions上取得33%的绝对提升（Y. Wang et al., 2023）。同时伴随幻觉扩散、模型崩溃、概念漂移与高计算成本等特征性失败。

Conclusion: 上述失败并非孤立技术缺陷，而是源自LLM与人类认知的结构性不对称；该框架可给出关于LbT何时成功或失败的可检验命题，并为设计更可靠的自主学 习系统提供结构化基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly exhibit capabilities that resemble human Learning by Thinking (LbT), the acquisition of new insight through internal processes such as explanation and reasoning, without new external input. A systematic account of the cognitive mechanisms behind these capabilities, and of their limits, remains underdeveloped. This paper proposes a Cognitive LbT Framework that maps four mechanisms (Reflective Reasoning, Synthetic Memory Formation, Internal Simulation, and Conceptual Self-Organization) onto computational processes in LLMs. Drawing on representative methods, including chain-of-thought prompting, self-consistency, self-reflection, Tree-of-Thoughts search, Self-Instruct, and self-taught reasoning, the analysis argues that selected methods can functionally approximate aspects of these mechanisms and can improve performance on some tasks without additional labeled examples, although this does not always constitute durable learning in model parameters. For example, Self-Instruct generated approximately 52,000 instructions from 175 seed tasks and reported a 33% absolute improvement on Super-NaturalInstructions (Y. Wang et al., 2023). The same mechanisms also produce characteristic failures, including hallucination diffusion, model collapse, concept drift, and high computational cost. These failures are not isolated technical defects but follow from four structural asymmetries between LLMs and human cognition: the identity of generator and monitor, closed learning loops without external grounding, symbolic rather than sensorimotor simulation, and the cost of inference-time computation. The framework yields testable propositions about when LbT in LLMs should succeed or fail and offers a structured basis for designing more reliable autonomous learning systems.

</details>


### [98] [An Energy-Based Perspective on Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14383)
*Bo Dai,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该教程以能量模型（EBM）为统一视角，提出任何EBM由能量函数、推断过程与学习方法三要素决定，并论证判别模型、GAN、扩散模型、对比学习、强化学习等众多方法本质上都是在这三要素（尤其在配分函数处理与采样方式）上不同的EBM，从而将机器学习各分支纳入同一框架并指导新算法设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献通常把EBM仅视为众多模型族之一。作者希望超越单纯介绍EBM，用EBM作为单一透镜连接机器学习的各个侧面，解释并组织已有方法，同时为设计与模型组合提供原理性框架。

Method: 以 p_θ(x)∝exp(-E_θ(x)) 定义分布，将EBM分解为三要素：能量（模型表示什么）、推断（采样或优化能量）、学习（拟合能量到数据）。系统阐述采样、变分推断，以及极大似然、噪声对比估计、伪似然、得分匹配、对抗训练等学习方法，并指出它们共享统一的对比结构；随后用该三要素框架重新诠释各类模型。

Result: 证明多种学习方法具有共同的对比结构；展示判别模型、GAN、归一化流、扩散与自回归模型、对比学习、word2vec、BERT、去噪自编码器、扩散表示、EBM策略与世界模型强化学习、逆强化学习、规划以及带验证器的推断期计算，均可视为在三要素选择（主要是配分函数处理与采样方式）上不同的EBM。

Conclusion: EBM三要素视角能解释现有方法、将其组织进统一框架，并通过替换其中某一要素而保持其余要素来启发新算法；能量组合可作为推断阶段组合已学习模型的原理性手段。

Abstract: Energy-based models (EBMs) define a distribution through a scalar energy, $p_θ(x)\propto\exp(-E_θ(x))$, and are usually presented as one model family among many. This tutorial has a broader goal: beyond introducing EBMs, it uses them as a single lens that connects the different aspects of machine learning. Any EBM is specified by three choices, the energy, which determines what the model represents; the inference procedure, which samples from or optimizes the energy; and the learning method, which fits the energy to data. We first develop these components, including sampling, variational inference, and learning by maximum likelihood, noise contrastive estimation, pseudolikelihood, score matching, and adversarial training, and show that the learning methods share one contrastive structure. We then show that discriminative models, generative models such as GANs, normalizing flows, diffusion and autoregressive models, representation learning methods such as contrastive learning, word2vec, BERT, denoising auto-encoders, and diffusion representations, reinforcement learning with EBM policies and world models, inverse reinforcement learning, and planning, as well as inference-time computation with verifiers, are all EBMs that differ in these three choices, mostly in how they handle the partition function and sampling. This view explains existing methods, organizes them into a common framework, and suggests new algorithms by replacing one component while keeping the others, with composition of energies as a principled way to combine learned models at inference time.

</details>


### [99] [Bits or permutations? Spending a fixed inference budget on weight precision or on option-order averaging in small local language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14413)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Logesh L,Aliyas Ahmad Misri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文研究权重量化（减少比特数）与选项顺序平均（多次重排）这两种开销手段的相互作用：量化是否加剧答案槽位偏置，以及在固定预算下"加比特"与"加重排"孰更划算。结论是6比特量化不损精度，且应优先用6比特权重、余下预算用于至多四次单遍循环重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特权重存储可省内存，多次重排平均可降低对选项顺序的敏感性，但两者组合的效果未知：量化可能放大答案槽位偏置从而削弱重排平均的收益。同时缺少在统一预算下比较"比特"与"重排遍数"的开销框架，且此前无关于量化与选项顺序联合研究的文献。

Method: 在Qwen3-0.6B与Qwen3-1.7B上模拟round-to-nearest权重量化（8/6/4/3比特，fp16为参照）；于ARC-Easy、ARC-Challenge、MMLU、HellaSwag各300题上从A–D字母的logits读取答案；用答案槽位偏置（slot skew）与翻转率度量顺序敏感性，并以同权值SNR的高斯噪声作为对照；对四次循环重排取平均；以"权值读取字节数×遍数"作为机器无关的代价单位，做代价匹配对比。

Result: 6比特相对fp16未见精度损失（甚至分别高+1.8与+0.9点，作者未作解释），8比特下降-0.7与-0.4点；4比特下槽位偏置由0.15升至0.53（0.6B）、由0.07升至0.12（1.7B），而等权值SNR高斯噪声对照给出0.15/0.37与0.19，故无法判定舍入是否带来超出等功率噪声的额外槽位偏置；翻转率上升但仍低于精度匹配的独立均匀误差基线；4比特下平均四次循环顺序在0.6B上恢复+12.6点（fp16为+5.3）。代价匹配对中，仅当权重不少于6比特时，增加重排次序才优于增加比特；决策表归结为：用6比特权重，余下预算用于至多四次单遍循环重排。

Conclusion: 在固定预算下，先保6比特权重、再把余量投给少量循环重排，是更省的选择；量化对顺序偏置的影响未被明确证实超出等功率噪声。局限在于仅两个小模型、仅模拟、仅最简单的舍入量化器，结论的外推需谨慎。

Abstract: Local language models can save memory by storing weights in fewer bits, and they can reduce sensitivity to the order of answer options by averaging a few reorderings. We ask whether the first makes the second worse, and which is the cheaper way to spend a fixed budget. On Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B with simulated round-to-nearest weight quantization (8, 6, 4 and 3 bits, fp16 reference), read from the logits of the letters A to D on ARC-Easy, ARC-Challenge, MMLU and HellaSwag (300 items each), 6 bits lose no accuracy against fp16 (they score +1.8 (+1.0 to +2.5) and +0.9 (+0.3 to +1.5) points higher, which we do not explain), and 8 bits change it by -0.7 (-1.1 to -0.3) and -0.4 (-0.7 to -0.2) points. At 4 bits the answer-slot skew rises from 0.15 to 0.53 (0.6B) and from 0.07 to 0.12 (1.7B); Gaussian noise at the same weight SNR gives 0.15 and 0.37 (two draws) and 0.19 (one draw), so the control is inconclusive on whether rounding adds slot skew beyond equal-power noise. Flip rates rise but stay below an accuracy-matched baseline of independent uniform errors. Averaging four cyclic orders recovers +12.6 points at 4 bits for 0.6B (+5.3 at fp16). We price bits and passes in one machine-independent unit, weight bytes read times passes. In cost-matched pairs, extra orders beat extra bits only when the weights have 6 or more bits, and the decision table reduces to: use 6-bit weights and spend the rest of the budget on up to four orders, one pass each. Everything is simulated on two small models with the simplest quantizer; a title-level search found no earlier study of quantization together with option order.

</details>


### [100] [Precision on Demand: Escalating Weight Bits at Uncertain Decoding Steps in a Small Local Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14418)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Logesh L,Aliyas Ahmad Misri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究"顶部4位解码+不确定性门控升级到8位"的混合精度分配策略：在重放步骤上熵/边际门控对argmax翻转的判别AUC达0.93，但150题实测中门控与全低/全高精度均无显著差异，结论仅为"有前景但未证实"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地解码通常对整个生成过程固定单一权重量化精度。本文尝试更细粒度的算力分配：默认用8位权重的顶部4位解码，仅在低精度输出分布不确定（熵超阈值或top-2边际过小）时，将该步重算为8位，从而在相同权重字节预算下提升质量。

Method: 模拟量化，模型Qwen3-0.6B，基准GSM8K，成本以每token读取的权重字节计。门控信号为低精度分布的熵与top-2边际。评估分三层：(1) 36724个重放步骤上，用AUC衡量门控对argmax翻转的排序能力，并与同升级率的随机门控期望比较；(2) 150道题的在线实验，比较全高、全低与熵门控三种配置，用McNemar检验与配对差值bootstrap区间；(3) 同一成本模型下"verified speculation"的KL对比。

Result: 重放层面：熵门控对argmax翻转AUC=0.93；15%升级预算下熵门控捕获78%翻转、边际门控94%，远高于随机门控的15%期望。在线层面：全高64.0%、全低55.3%（McNemar p=0.053）；16%升级率的熵门控62.7%，消耗全高87%的权重字节，其配对差相对全高为−1.3（−8.0~+5.3）分、相对全低为+7.3（0.0~+14.7）分（p=0.080）。

Conclusion: 门控在离线判别指标上显著有效，但150题的在线样本量不足以把门控与全高、全低任一端区分开，尚不能宣称收益。且随机在线对照、thinking-mode阶段规则、第二模型、在线KL均未运行，属未验证的推测性结论。需扩大样本并补齐对照与跨模型验证。

Abstract: Local decoding usually fixes one weight precision for the whole generation. We study a different allocation: store 8-bit weights, decode with their top 4 bits, and redo a step at 8 bits only when the low-precision output distribution looks uncertain (entropy above a threshold or a small top-two margin). Quantization is simulated, the model is Qwen3-0.6B, and the benchmark is GSM8K. Cost is counted in weight bytes read per token. On 36724 replayed steps the entropy of the low pass ranks argmax flips with AUC 0.93. At a 15% escalation budget it catches 78% of flips and the margin gate 94%, against the 15% expected for a random gate of equal rate (a computed expectation, not a run). In 150 live problems all-high scores 64.0% and all-low 55.3% (McNemar p=0.053). An entropy gate with a 16% escalation rate scores 62.7% at 0.87 of the all-high weight bytes. Its paired difference is -1.3 (-8.0 to +5.3) points to all-high and +7.3 (+0.0 to +14.7) points to all-low (p=0.080), so the live data do not separate the gate from either end. Verified speculation reaches zero KL at similar bytes in the same cost model. A live random control, a thinking-mode phase rule, a second model and live KL were not run.

</details>


### [101] [Evaluating LLM-Generated World Models for Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14419)
*Souhaila Djaffal,Amna Jamal,Gagana Madhugiri Devaraja,Anidipta Pal,Jobst Heitzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM能否将开放式自然语言情境形式化为部分可观测随机博弈（POSG）世界模型，并指出"机械有效性"是必要但不充分的标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化世界模型可支撑规划、验证与安全推理，但从开放式自然语言（尤其多智能体场景）中构建十分困难，且往往存在多种合理形式化。核心问题是：机械上可执行是否足以保证模型忠实反映了原意决策问题。

Method: 构建含44个场景（博弈、情境、文化三类）的基准，从三个维度评估生成的世界模型：机械有效性、参照相对的置换不变结构一致性、语义准则；在三条实验轨道中共分析410次首轮生成。

Result: 参照博弈轨道中135个模型有134个通过严格机械审计，但平均置换不变结构F1仅0.366，仅28/134与作者参照完全一致；文化轨道中显式点名国家会改变多数LLM生成的结构表示，而自动语义分数变化很小。

Conclusion: 可执行性对LLM生成的世界模型是必要但不充分条件；机械有效的模型在下游神经符号推理与规划所依赖的结构上仍可能显著偏离。

Abstract: Formal world models can support planning, verification, and safety reasoning, but constructing them from open-ended natural-language situations is difficult, particularly in multi-agent settings where several formalizations may be plausible. We study whether five LLMs can generate such world models as partially observable stochastic games and whether mechanical validity is sufficient to establish that the resulting model faithfully represents the intended decision problem.
