Result: 三类零样本涌现能力均可泛化至分布外复合物；各能力在最大规模处呈连续指标上的急剧跃升，而预训练损失平滑下降，说明仅凭基于损失的缩放律无法预测涌现能力的出现时机；冻结表征的结合与亲和力预测达到SOTA且比共折叠方法快约五个数量级；全新化合物剂量-反应命中率61.5%，得到此前未被报道可抑制这些蛋白的结构新颖分子。

Conclusion: 多模态生物学基础模型可在无监督、无结构坐标条件下产生层级化零样本涌现能力，且表现出与损失曲线解耦的"相变式"缩放行为；这提示基于损失的缩放律不足以预测能力涌现，并为高效、可泛化的分子互作建模与先导化合物发现提供新路径。

Abstract: Foundation models trained on large unlabeled corpora develop emergent capabilities, tasks that go beyond the training objective. We extend this paradigm to multimodal biology with Bonbon, a foundation model for molecular interactions trained on approximately 3.2 trillion tokens of paired protein-ligand sequences. From a single representation space, Bonbon exhibits three zero-shot emergent capabilities at hierarchical levels of resolution: functional (mechanism of action), residue (binding site localization, including orthosteric/allosteric discrimination), and bond (covalent versus non-covalent engagement). All three capabilities generalize to out-of-distribution complexes. Bonbon was trained on protein-ligand sequences alone, without any task-specific supervision and without structural coordinates. We conducted controlled scaling experiments across six model scales from 42M to 1.6B parameters; every capability evaluated shows a sharp jump at the largest scale on continuous metrics, even as pretraining loss decreases smoothly. This demonstrates that loss-based scaling laws alone are insufficient to predict when zero-shot emergent capabilities become available. The capabilities reported here emerge after training at a 1:2,000 parameter-to-token ratio to accommodate the sparsity of paired biological interaction data. The same frozen representations achieve state-of-the-art binding and affinity prediction at five orders of magnitude greater speed than co-folding methods. In prospective wet-lab validation across nine disease targets spanning seven protein families, Bonbon achieves a 61.5% dose-response hit rate on de novo compounds, yielding structurally novel molecules not previously reported to inhibit these proteins, including for out-of-distribution targets.

</details>


### [236] [Continual-ARC: Measuring Skill Acquisition over a Working Lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14505)
*Daniel Lenton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Continual-ARC 终身学习流基准（由三届 ARC-AGI 任务构成），统一评分记忆型与权重型学习器，实验表明现有 agent harness 的持久化机制几乎无助于记忆与泛化，而权重学习在未见任务上收益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 continual learning 基准存在三点局限：只覆盖单一应用领域、每个实例只出现一次、只假设单一家族的学习器，导致基于记忆与基于权重的系统难以在同一尺度上比较。

Method: 构建 Continual-ARC：任务来自三届 ARC-AGI，无需领域知识；任务在休眠间隔后以新实例重现，同时新任务持续到来。学习器可在每实例上付费购买示范、提交答案或在 harness 内行动，错误提交可重试但需付费；按消耗的反馈预算比例计分。跨实例间任意保存信息，使记忆型与权重型学习器同尺度评估。流结束后冻结学习器，在已见任务的新实例（测记忆）与未见任务（测泛化）上测试。实验采用冻结权重的 agent harness（有/无持久化），在 Qwen3.5-9B 与 GLM-5.3 Flash 上运行，并与两个提示基线、一个程序归纳方法及模仿 GLM-5.3 Flash 训练的 Qwen3.5-9B 权重对照。

Result: 除非提供显式专家指导，harness 的持久化一般不提升记忆效果；在所有测试模型上，多数 harness 方法的学习效果仍低于"直接缓存各任务示范"的简单基线；没有任何 harness 方法在未见任务上获得增益，而基于权重的学习表现出显著提升。

Conclusion: 现有 agent harness 的持续学习能力有限，权重型学习在跨任务泛化上更具潜力；该基准为未来"训练模型驱动 harness 进入新领域"的权重型持续学习研究提供了统一评测平台。

Abstract: Agent harnesses have emerged as a promising mechanism for continual learning, alongside test-time parametric learning. However, the current benchmarks have several limitations: they generally focus on a single applied domain, serve each instance only once, and assume a single family of learner, so memory-based and weight-based systems are rarely compared on the same scale. We propose Continual-ARC, a lifelong stream built from the three ARC-AGI editions, whose tasks need no domain knowledge. The same tasks return after dormant gaps with fresh instances, while new tasks keep arriving. On each instance, the learner may pay for demonstrations, submit answers, or act inside the harness, with retries permitted on incorrect submissions, which are paid for. Each instance is scored based on the share of this feedback budget consumed. Since a learner may keep anything between instances, memory-based and weight-based systems are scored on the same scale. After the stream, the learner is frozen and tested on fresh instances of the tasks it met, which measures memorization, and on unseen tasks, which measures generalization. We run an agent harness with frozen weights, with and without persistence, on Qwen3.5-9B and GLM-5.3 Flash, comparing them with two prompting floors, a program-induction method and Qwen3.5-9B's weights trained to imitate GLM-5.3 Flash. Persistence in the harness generally does not improve memorization unless provided with explicit expert guidance. Most harness-based methods still learn less than a floor that simply caches each task's demonstrations, across all models we tested. No harness-based method gains on unseen tasks, whereas weight-based learning shows substantial gains. This opens the door to future weight-based continual learning that could teach models to drive harnesses in new domains.

</details>


### [237] [When Does the Real Wiring Win? Testing connectome structure-function claims against unmeasured physiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14508)
*Yeyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 连接组约束模型常被用来论证真实接线优于打乱/重连接线，但这类比较混淆了"接线"与"模型调参"两个因素。作者区分三种估计量（P1/P2/P3），用合成真值、安慰剂零模型和三个果蝇电路模型量化已知机器学习偏差对生物学结论的影响：部分结论在各协议下稳健，另一些则被协议翻转（如蘑菇体Kenyon细胞输出接线在P1/P2下胜出、在P3下反而落败）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"连接组决定功能"的论证依赖真实接线模型优于shuffled/rewired对照，但连接组只固定拓扑，不固定兴奋性、增益、可塑性速率等参数，建模者常反复调参直至真实接线模型"跑通"，从而引入欠调基线、继承权重、赢家诅咒等偏差，使结构与功能的比较结论不可靠。作者旨在分离这些偏差并测量其对生物学结论的实际影响。

Method: 提出三层估计量划分：P1为在真实接线上调好生理参数后迁移到零模型；P2为每种接线各自独立调参；P3为P2进一步约束到与独立数据一致的生理参数范围。使用合成真值数据、安慰剂零模型（placebo nulls）以及三个果蝇电路模型（蘑菇体、flyvis等）进行系统比较，并做held-out复跑、十倍/五十倍重训练等稳健性检验，报告95%置信区间。

Result: 稳健结论：隔离的奖赏/惩罚多巴胺通路改善学习；头方向地形图是持续性bump所必需。非稳健结论：蘑菇体Kenyon细胞输出接线对跨亚型identity shuffle，P1与P2下领先0.09，P3下反而落后0.09（CI排除零，held-out复跑一致）；判决由活跃Kenyon细胞中alpha'beta'细胞占比决定，P1选中低占比，而影像学隐含约44%时真实接线模型学不会，暴露的是模型不足而非果蝇接线缺陷。flyvis中P1惩罚镜像连接组（其构造解误差中位数在真实误差0.07以内），50/50成员中惩罚强于空间shuffle；该排序在十倍重训练下保持，五十倍时依赖checkpoint。

Conclusion: 结构与功能的比较高度依赖调参协议：P1易产生欠调基线偏差并放大赢家诅咒，可能把模型不足误判为接线缺陷。可信的结构性论断需要匹配的优化、数据约束的生理参数，以及在该参数区域内仍能工作的模型。

Abstract: Connectome-constrained models are often used to argue that a measured wiring diagram outperforms shuffled or rewired wiring, and hence that structure shapes function. Such comparisons can confound wiring with how the models are tuned: a connectome fixes who connects to whom, not the excitability, gains or plasticity rates the model also needs, and modellers often tune these until the real-wiring model works. We separate three estimands: physiology tuned on the real wiring and transferred to nulls (P1), each wiring tuned on its own (P2), and P2 restricted to physiology consistent with independent data (P3). The biases involved are known in machine learning (under-tuned baselines, inherited weights, winner's curse); we measure what they do to biological conclusions, using synthetic ground truth, placebo nulls and three Drosophila circuit models. Some claims survive every protocol we test: segregated reward and punishment dopamine improves learning, and head-direction topography is required for a persistent bump. Others do not. In the mushroom body, the real Kenyon-cell output wiring beats a cross-subtype identity shuffle by 0.09 under P1 and P2 but loses to it by 0.09 under P3 (95% CIs exclude zero; held-out runs confirm). The verdict is set by the share of alpha'beta' cells among active Kenyon cells, which P1 selects low; at the share implied by imaging (about 44%) the real-wiring model fails to learn, exposing a model inadequacy rather than a deficit of the fly's wiring. In flyvis, P1 penalises a mirrored connectome, which has a constructed solution within 0.07 (median) of the real error, more than a spatially shuffled one in 50 of 50 members; the ranking survives ten-fold longer retraining but depends on the checkpoint at fifty-fold. A credible structural claim needs matched optimisation, data-constrained physiology and a model that still works there.

</details>


### [238] [Pyrite: Building and Benchmarking Protein-Ligand Pose Ranking Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14511)
*Megha Srivastava,Michael H. Lim,Allison M. Keys,Pranav Murugan,Nate Gruver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Pyrite成对构象排序基准（1,500对蛋白-配体复合物构象），专家化学家近随机水平；基于结构化表示PyriteRep构建两个LLM智能体，准确率达67%与74%，超越专家，且LLM引导的特征发现优于直接推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代共折叠（cofolding）模型能生成多样候选构象，但可靠地对候选构象排序仍是瓶颈；同时需要检验人类专家与现有化学信号在此任务上的实际能力上限，并探索自动化排序方案。

Method: 构建Pyrite成对构象排序基准，含1,500个跨多样蛋白-配体复合物的构象对；由使用3D可视化与物理打分的专家化学家进行人类基线评估；提出PyriteRep结构化表示（分子几何、蛋白-配体相互作用、理化打分）；开发两个LLM智能体：PyriteReasoner（文本推理直接比较）与PyriteFeaturizer（生成可执行代码构造特征用于逻辑回归，可选引入LLM子调用派生特征）；并用GRPO对开放权重模型进行强化学习优化。

Result: 专家表现接近随机；PyriteReasoner最高约67%准确率，PyriteFeaturizer最高约74%，均超过专家；LLM引导的特征发现总体优于直接推理；GRPO使开放权重模型达到或超过闭源前沿模型。

Conclusion: Pyrite可作为构象排序能力的挑战性测试平台；LLM引导的特征发现是解释并融合计算化学工具输出的有前景范式。

Abstract: Predicting how a ligand binds to its target is central to small-molecule drug discovery. Modern cofolding models can generate diverse candidate poses, but reliably ranking these candidates remains challenging. We introduce Pyrite, a pairwise pose-ranking benchmark comprising 1,500 pose pairs across diverse protein-ligand complexes. Expert chemists equipped with 3D visualization tools and physics-based scores perform near chance, highlighting the difficulty of interpreting available chemical signals. To investigate automated ranking, we introduce PyriteRep, a structured representation of molecular geometry, protein-ligand interactions, and physicochemical scores, and develop two LLM-based agents: PyriteReasoner, which directly compares poses through text-based reasoning, and PyriteFeaturizer, which generates executable code to construct features for logistic regression, optionally incorporating features derived from LLM subcalls. These agents achieve up to 67% and 74% accuracy, respectively, exceeding observed expert performance. LLM-guided feature discovery generally outperforms direct reasoning, while Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) improves open-weight models, enabling them to match or surpass closed-source frontier models in our evaluations. These results establish Pyrite as a challenging test of pose-ranking ability and highlight LLM-guided feature discovery as a promising approach to interpret and combine the outputs of computational chemistry tools.

</details>


### [239] [AutoScaffold: AI-Guided Scaffolding for Reinforcement Learning of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14515)
*Zijian Zhang,Rizhen Hu,Jiangweizhi Peng,Shiyang Li,Sujay Bhatt,Sihan Zeng,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoScaffold：用冻结教师模型分析学生RLVR训练经验，自动生成并自适应调整"策略型脚手架"与"困难任务提示"，评估时移除脚手架，在ALFWorld、搜索问答、数学推理、GPU kernel生成四个领域均优于对应RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽有丰富训练经验，但结果奖励把每次尝试压缩为单一数值，大量信息被浪费；训练时脚手架（提示中的策略/提示）可把信息转为指导，但其设计与调整依赖人工且高度领域专用。

Method: 提出统一的AI-for-AI框架AutoScaffold：冻结教师周期性检查学生训练经验，诊断瓶颈，并以两种形式设计脚手架——跨任务类共享的策略与针对个别困难任务的提示；随学生学习进度动态调整内容与支持量；几乎不需领域知识、训练中无需人工干预，评估时移除脚手架。

Result: 无脚手架评估下，四个领域均优于对应RL基线：ALFWorld成功率+7.2点，QA平均精确匹配+4.0点，GPU kernel生成中"正确且快于PyTorch参考"的任务比例最高+5.0点；数学上以约1/3学生训练步数超过QuestA报告准确率。

Conclusion: 分析显示教师能为每类任务识别不同失败模式，并随学生进步将脚手架从分步流程转向针对性修补；修订后的脚手架比沿用旧脚手架或无脚手架更能促进学生学��。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) produces rich training experience, yet outcome rewards reduce each attempt to a single number and leave most of this information unused. Training-time scaffolds, such as strategies or hints added to the prompt during training, offer a natural way to turn this information into guidance, but their design and adaptation are largely left to humans and tailored to specific domains. We introduce AutoScaffold, a unified AI-for-AI framework that turns a student's training experience into adaptive scaffolds, with minimal domain-specific information and no human intervention during training. A frozen teacher periodically examines this experience, diagnoses bottlenecks, and designs scaffolds in two forms: (i) strategies shared across a class of tasks and (ii) hints for individual hard tasks, adjusting the content and amount of support as the student learns. The scaffolds are removed at evaluation. We apply the same framework to ALFWorld, search-based QA, mathematical reasoning, and GPU kernel generation. When evaluated without scaffolds, students trained with AutoScaffold outperform those trained with the corresponding RL baselines in all four domains, with gains of 7.2 points in ALFWorld success and 4.0 points in average QA exact match. On GPU kernel generation, AutoScaffold increases the fraction of tasks with a correct kernel that runs faster than the PyTorch reference by up to 5.0 points. On math, our student surpasses QuestA's reported accuracy using approximately one-third as many student training steps. Analyses show that the teacher identifies a different failure in each task class and shifts its scaffolds from step-by-step workflows to targeted fixes as the student improves, and that its revised scaffolds help the student learn more than keeping the previous scaffold or using no scaffold.

</details>


### [240] [TorchCurves: Differentiable Parametric Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14516)
*Alex Shtoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TorchCurves 是一个 PyTorch 库，将向量值参数曲线（B 样条或 Legendre 多项式基表示）作为可学习原语，支持对输入与系数双向求导，并提供可训练模块、可复用基模块与函数式接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让曲线系数可与周边模型联合优化或由另一网络生成，从而把连续特征嵌入、Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络（KAN）与函数型回归等统一到同一套可微曲线抽象下。

Method: 核心算子将批量标量输入映射为曲线上的点，基函数采用 B 样条或 Legendre 多项式；实现上结合经典求值算法与向量化 PyTorch 运算，不使用自定义硬件内核，强调可读性与可维护性。提供三类接口：可训练模块、可复用基模块、函数式接口。

Result: 计时实验在多种问题规模下将曲线求值与 NumPy、SciPy 对比，并将 KAN 训练与 EfficientKAN 对比，结果表明该通用实现在一部分规模区间计算上实用，而在另一些区间开销显著。

Conclusion: TorchCurves 以通用、易维护的方式统一可微参数曲线建模，代价是在部分场景引入额外计算开销；代码开源于 GitHub。

Abstract: We present TorchCurves, a PyTorch library that treats vector-valued parametric curves as learnable primitives. Its central operation maps batches of scalar inputs to points on curves represented in B-spline or Legendre polynomial bases, with differentiation through both inputs and coefficients. Curve coefficients can be optimized jointly with the surrounding model or produced by another network. The library provides trainable modules, reusable basis modules, and a functional interface, supporting applications ranging from continuous feature embeddings to Kolmogorov-Arnold networks and functional regression. Its implementation combines classical evaluation algorithms with vectorized PyTorch operations, emphasizing readability and maintainability without custom hardware kernels. Timing experiments compare curve evaluation against NumPy and SciPy and KAN training against EfficientKAN across a range of problem sizes. The results illustrate where this generality remains computationally practical and where overhead is substantial. Library is available on GitHub at https://github.com/alexshtf/torchcurves.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [DocFS: File Systems as Agentic Reasoning Environments for Complex Multimodal Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12957)
*Bingdian Zhang,Zixuan Li,Long Bai,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 DocFS：将原始文档重组为文件系统（目录对应章节层级、显式链接连接文本与图/表/公式），配合文件系统原生工具（导航、搜索、阅读）使智能体迭代探索并定位证据；在 MMLongBench-Doc 与 FinRAGBench-V 上分别领先最强基线 2.9 与 4.3 个百分点，推理速度最高快 10.3 倍、token 用量最多减少 10.1 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 VLM 的智能体通过与环境迭代交互解决复杂任务，其效果高度依赖环境如何暴露信息并提供作用于该信息的工具。面向复杂多模态文档问答构建有效环境仍具挑战：文档通常跨多页，多模态内容分布在不同层级章节中并由跨模态关联连接；现有方法仅将文档整理为结构化表示，只解决了环境的"信息侧"，缺乏与结构对齐的工具，限制了智能体对结构的充分利用。作者观察到文件系统天然兼具结构组织与对齐工具，由此获得设计动机。

Method: DocFS 是一种基于文件系统的智能体框架：将原始文档重组为文件系统，其中目录镜像章节层级结构，并通过显式链接将文本与图、表、公式等模态内容关联起来。在此基础上，智能体可借助成熟的文件系统工具（导航、搜索、阅读）迭代探索文档结构、定位相关证据，并理解细粒度的局部信息。

Result: 搭配 Qwen3.5-Plus，DocFS 在 MMLongBench-Doc 与 FinRAGBench-V 上取得当前最优性能，分别超过最强基线 2.9 与 4.3 个百分点；推理阶段最高提速 10.3 倍，token 用量最多减少 10.1 倍。

Conclusion: 文件系统是组织多模态长文档并为其配备对齐工具的有效环境范式，可同时提升多模态文档问答的准确性与推理效率，实现结构、工具与智能体探索行为的一致性。

Abstract: VLM-based agents solve complex tasks by iteratively interacting with environments, and their effectiveness depends heavily on how the environment exposes information and provides tools for acting on it. However, constructing an effective environment for complex multimodal document question answering remains challenging: documents usually span many pages, with multimodal contents distributed across hierarchical sections and linked by cross-modal associations, while existing methods organize documents into structured representations that address only the information side of the environment and lack tools aligned with the structure, limiting agents' ability to fully exploit it. We observe that file systems naturally combine structure organization with aligned tools: directories and files encode document structure, while file operations provide rich tools to access file contents. Building on this insight, we propose DocFS, a file-system-based agentic framework that reorganizes raw documents as file systems, where directories mirror the section hierarchy and explicit links connect text with figures, tables, and equations. Based on well-established file system tools such as navigation, search, and reading, agents can iteratively explore the document structure, locate relevant evidence, and understand fine-grained local information. Paired with Qwen3.5-Plus, DocFS achieves state-of-the-art performance on MMLongBench-Doc and FinRAGBench-V, outperforming the strongest baselines by 2.9 and 4.3 percentage points, respectively, and is up to 10.3x faster with up to 10.1x fewer tokens than the strongest baseline during reasoning.

</details>


### [242] [Exploiting Feature-invariant Scaling Laws for Efficient A/B Experimentation in Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13539)
*Zhiliang Chen,Sammy Nammari,Jiahao Su,Xiaoqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文发现现代推荐模型存在"特征不变缩放律"(feature-invariant scaling)，即不同特征改动下模型性能变化均遵循可预测的缩放规律；据此提出基于统计推断的高效方法，仅用小规模实验即可带置信区间预测A/B实验的全规模效果，在Wukong、HSTU和LLM-based三类模型、40余项改动上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统依赖A/B实验评估新架构、优化方法和数据特征，但全规模实验（含在线数据的全模型训练）计算开销过高，而单次小规模实验的结果又常常具有误导性，因此亟需一种能低成本、可靠预测全规模实验效果的方法。

Method: 提出并验证"特征不变缩放律"现象；基于该缩放律构建统计推断框架，用小规模实验数据拟合缩放曲线，并对全规模A/B实验效果给出带置信区间的预测，从而高效筛选中性或退化的特征改动。

Result: 在Wukong、HSTU和LLM-based推荐模型三类模型族、40余项架构/优化/数据改动上，方法能准确区分有益、中性与退化的特征改动，仅消耗全规模评估所需算力的一小部分；并发现若干改动可使HSTU在MovieLens-20M上的SOTA性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: 缩放律可被实际利用，为工业级推荐系统的改进提供计算高效的决策依据，显著降低特征迭代的实验成本。

Abstract: Researchers and engineers improve recommendation systems by performing A/B experiments with new model architectures, optimization methods, and data features. However, running full-scale experiments (e.g., full-model training with online data) for every feature change is prohibitively computationally intensive, while the result from a single small-scale experiment is often misleading. To address this challenge, we show that modern recommendation models exhibit feature-invariant scaling: the change of a model's performance follows a predictable scaling-law even when different feature changes are applied to the baseline model. We introduce an efficient method that exploits scaling-laws and uses statistical inference to predict (with confidence intervals) the full-scale effect of an A/B experiment in recommendation systems. This enables practitioners to efficiently filter out neutral or regressive feature changes with only small-scale experimentation. We validate our method on three distinct recommendation model families---Wukong, HSTU, and LLM-based recommendation models---across more than 40 architectural, optimization, and data changes. Our method accurately distinguishes beneficial feature changes from neutral and regressive ones while using only a fraction of the compute required for full-scale evaluation. It also identifies several changes that improve HSTU's state-of-the-art performance on MovieLens-20M. Our results demonstrate that scaling laws can be practically exploited to make compute-efficient decisions for improving industrial-scale recommendation systems.

</details>


### [243] [Auditing LLM Pseudo-Label Distillation for Review Informativeness at E-Commerce Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13797)
*M. Emin Dogan,Cansu Er,Aysenur Kulunk,M. Furkan Eseoglu,H. Bahadir Sahin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究从 Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite 到紧凑编码器的硬标签蒸馏，在 534 万条土耳其电商评论上同时考察"教师保真度"与"编辑一致性"两类评价，发现 H3 类目前缀的上下文效应依赖评测人群，教师保真度本身不足以证明编辑对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型蒸馏到小模型通常以教师标签的复现程度（教师保真度）作为唯一验收标准，但该指标是否等价于人工编辑的接受程度尚不明确；同时，上下文前缀（如类目前缀）在分布内外的收益是否稳定也缺少检验。

Method: 在 5.34M 条土耳其市场评论上做硬标签蒸馏。设计两套互补评测：(1) 严格的新 SKU 时间切分测试集衡量教师保真度；(2) 由一名公司标注员独立完成的、对教师标签盲的审计，衡量编辑一致性（16,000 条按时间排序的审计子集）。对比加入 H3 类目前缀的效果，并比较 CORN 与交叉熵两种损失下的配对差异。

Result: H3 类目前缀在完整新 SKU 测试集上可小幅提升教师保真度；但在 16,000 条时间序审计子集上，它反而降低与 Gemini 及标注员的一致性，且 CORN 的配对差异比交叉熵更明显。离线配置下，单张 L4 的学生流水线比教师流水线快 50.2 倍。

Conclusion: 上下文前缀的效应取决于评测人群：保真度导向的测试与编辑导向的审计给出相反结论。因此教师保真度不能单独作为编辑对齐的代理指标，蒸馏评估应引入独立的、对教师盲的人工审计。

Abstract: We study hard-label distillation from Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite to compact encoders on 5.34 million Turkish marketplace reviews. A strict new-SKU temporal test measures teacher fidelity; a separate, teacher-label-blind audit by one company annotator measures editorial agreement. An H3 category prefix slightly improves teacher fidelity on the full new-SKU test. On the 16,000-review chronological audit subset, it instead reduces agreement with both Gemini and the annotator, with a clearer paired difference for CORN than for cross entropy. Thus the context effect depends on the evaluation population, and teacher fidelity alone does not establish editorial alignment. Under the measured offline configurations, a single-L4 student pipeline processes reviews 50.2 times faster than the teacher pipeline.

</details>


### [244] [Controlled Knowledge Engineering for Taxonomy Induction with Tool-Grounded LLM Agents and Runtime-Adaptive Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13966)
*Jonas Limniatis,Bennet Schaarmann,Georg Reuber,Robert Stahlbock*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TIGA 将分类体系归纳形式化为在显式状态与受限动作空间上的迭代决策过程，借助可执行的编辑工具、记录工具调用/理由/结果的记忆、以及运行时自适应组织策略，在受限候选实体集上构建完整、结构合法、语义连贯的分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类体系支撑语义搜索、推荐、产品目录组织与 RAG，但需从固定候选实体集自动构建，要求层级完整、结构合法、语义一致，而非仅文本合理。LLM 的自由形式迭代生成易出现实体遗漏或幻觉、非法层级关系、组织原则不一致等问题。

Method: 提出 TIGA（Taxonomy Induction with Tool-Grounded Agents）知识工程架构：（1）可执行的分类体系编辑工具；（2）交互记忆，记录工具调用、推理理由与执行结果；（3）运行时自适应组织策略，智能体在推理过程中自行修订；（4）可选的全局审查器，依据候选集与当前生效策略评估所构建的分类体系。在四个基准分类体系、两种 LLM 骨干上与单次提示和迭代提示基线对比，并做消融实验。

Result: 在多数评测配置下 TIGA 取得最佳结构质量，但并非在所有精度指标上全面领先；性能提升伴随显著更高的 token 消耗、API 调用量与推理时延。消融显示策略组件与交互记忆组件带来稳定收益，而全局审查的效果取决于数据集与可用修订预算。

Conclusion: 工具接地与运行时策略自适应是改善结构化知识表示受控构建的有效但高成本机制；TIGA 以显式状态、受限动作与记忆驱动的策略修订，缓解自由生成中的遗漏、幻觉与结构非法问题。

Abstract: Taxonomies are structured knowledge representations that support semantic search, recommendation, product catalog organization, and retrieval-augmented generation. Their automated construction from a fixed set of candidate entities requires more than plausible text generation: the resulting hierarchy must be complete, structurally valid, and semantically coherent. Large Language Models (LLMs) are promising for this task, but iterative free-form generation remains vulnerable to omitted or hallucinated entities, invalid hierarchical relations, and inconsistent organizational principles. We introduce TIGA (Taxonomy Induction with Tool-Grounded Agents), a knowledge-engineering architecture that formulates taxonomy induction as an iterative decision process over an explicit state and constrained action space. TIGA combines executable taxonomy-editing tools, an memory that records tool calls, rationales, and outcomes, and a runtime-adaptive organizational policy that the agent revises during inference. An optional global reviewer evaluates the constructed taxonomy against the candidate set and the active policy. We evaluate TIGA on four benchmark taxonomies using two LLM backbones and compare it with single-shot and iterative prompting-based baselines. TIGA achieves the strongest structural-quality results in most evaluated configurations, but does not dominate on every precision measure. The gains are accompanied by substantially higher token consumption, API-call volume, and inference time. Ablation results show consistent benefits from the policy and interaction-memory components, whereas the effect of global review depends on the dataset and the available revision budget. These findings characterize tool grounding and runtime policy adaptation as promising but costly mechanisms for improving the controlled construction of structured knowledge representations.

</details>


### [245] [Data Quality Challenges in Multimodal Tourism Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13972)
*Zehui Wang,Wolfram Höpken,Dietmar Jannach*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文关注多模态推荐系统中基础数据质量问题，以旅游推荐为场景，考察Yelp与Instagram真实数据中的虚假评论噪声、LLM幻觉内容、模态矛盾与人口统计偏差，并提出质量感知融合、受控LLM内容生成、用户群体差异化等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐研究多聚焦模型性能提升，却忽视社交媒体等多模态数据本身的质量问题，数据缺陷可能损害推荐系统的鲁棒性与可靠性。

Method: 以旅游推荐为应用场景，对来自Yelp和Instagram的真实数据集进行经验性考察，归纳实际遇到的数据质量挑战。

Result: 识别出四类关键问题：虚假评论带来的噪声、LLM生成内容的幻觉、不同模态间的信息矛盾，以及人口统计层面的偏差。

Conclusion: 呼吁将数据质量前置纳入多模态推荐设计，提出质量感知融合、结构化受控的LLM内容生成以抑制幻觉、以及用户群体差异化建模等方向，以提升系统的鲁棒性与可靠性。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems increasingly consider diverse data modalities to enhance model performance. However, the quality of the underlying multimodal data (e.g. data from social media platforms) has received limited attention so far. Focusing on tourism recommendation, we examine real-world challenges encountered when using datasets from Yelp and Instagram. Issues include noise from fake reviews, hallucinated content from LLMs, modality contradictions, and demographic bias. We outline future directions such as quality-aware fusion, structured and controlled LLM-based content generation to reduce hallucinations, and user group differentiation to improve the robustness and reliability of multimodal recommendation systems.

</details>


### [246] [LUMIR: Decoupled Multimodal Representation Learning from Heterogeneous Data for Next-POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14004)
*Zehui Wang,Wolfram Höpken,Dietmar Jannach*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LUMIR，一个基于对比学习的解耦式多模态表示学习框架：先用LLM将异构多源多模态信息语义统一到共享文本空间，再用交互感知融合机制建模跨模态交互，并以序列信号作为结构约束引导表示学习，从而获得可复用、与下游模型解耦的多模态表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态旅游推荐方法将多模态融合与特定下游模型的架构及优化目标紧耦合，限制了所学表示的通用性与可复用性。

Method: LUMIR采用对比学习实现解耦式表示学习：(1) 利用大语言模型将异构数据源采集的多模态信息语义统一至共享文本表示空间；(2) 设计交互感知融合机制建模跨模态交互；(3) 引入序列信号作为结构约束引导表示学习；表示学习与下游推荐目标分离。

Result: 在多模态POI数据集的下一兴趣点推荐设定下，跨多个序列推荐骨干网络进行实验，LUMIR在准确性指标与超越准确性指标上均稳定优于现有多模态融合基线。

Conclusion: 将表示学习与下游优化解耦，是学习可复用多模态表示的有效范式；LUMIR验证了该范式的普遍适用性与性能优势（代码已开源）。

Abstract: As multimodal tourism-related information collected from heterogeneous data sources has become increasingly available, learning effective representations has attracted attention for improving recommendation performance. Existing approaches often tightly couple multimodal fusion with the architecture and optimization objective of a specific downstream model, which limits the generality and reusability of the learned representations. To address these limitations, we propose LUMIR, a decoupled multimodal representation learning framework based on contrastive learning. Multimodal information collected from heterogeneous data sources is first semantically unified into a shared textual representation space using large language models (LLMs). An interaction-aware fusion mechanism is then designed to model cross-modal interactions, while sequential signals are incorporated as a structural constraint to guide representation learning. To evaluate the learned representations, experiments are conducted on a multimodal point-of-interest (POI) dataset under the next-POI recommendation setting across multiple sequential recommendation backbones. The results show that LUMIR consistently outperforms existing multimodal fusion baselines in both accuracy-oriented and beyond-accuracy metrics, demonstrating that decoupling representation learning from downstream optimization provides an effective paradigm for learning reusable multimodal representations. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/wzehui/LUMIR.

</details>


### [247] [From Show Programs to Linked Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14037)
*Clarisse Bardiot,Bernard Jacquemin,Jacob Hart,George Bruseker,Antonios Lagarias,Pierre-Carl Langlais,Brandon Farnsworth,Jeanne Fras*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一套将演出节目单转化为结构化、可互操作数据的工作流，融合视觉语言模型（VLM）、面向表演艺术的Linked Art本体自定义扩展与自动语义标注方法，并以法国国家图书馆藏阿维尼翁戏剧节节目单（1947年至今）为案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗产机构收藏的节目单数量庞大，无法逐件编目，长期难以获取且大多未数字化；现有方法难以支撑大规模、可互操作的表演艺术史研究。

Method: 构建端到端工作流：用VLM对异质遗产文档图像进行文本转录；扩展Linked Art本体以覆盖表演艺术领域；比较多种自动语义标注方案，并通过知识图谱约束大语言模型生成以抑制幻觉。

Result: 在阿维尼翁戏剧节节目单图像上，VLM文本转录准确率超过98%；以知识图谱约束LLM生成可显著减少语义标注中的幻觉。

Conclusion: 该工作流为大规模、可互操作的表演艺术史研究开辟了新可能，包括为表演艺术家编制"全集目录"（catalogues raisonnés）——这一在视觉艺术中常见、而表演艺术研究尚缺的学术形式。

Abstract: Many heritage institutions hold extensive collections of show programs. Too numerous to be cataloged individually, they remain difficult to access and are still largely undigitized. This paper presents a workflow for transforming such documents into structured, interoperable data by combining vision-language models (VLMs), a custom extension of the Linked Art ontology for the performing arts, and different approaches for automatic semantic data annotation. Using the Festival d'Avignon programs (1947--present), preserved at the Biblioth{è}que nationale de France, as a case study, we demonstrate how VLMs achieve more than 98% text transcription accuracy from heterogeneous heritage document images and how constraining large language model generation through a knowledge graph significantly reduces hallucinations in semantic annotation. We argue that this workflow opens new possibilities for large-scale, interoperable performing arts historiography, including the creation of catalogues raisonn{é}s for performing artists, a form of scholarship common in visual arts but absent in performing arts studies.

</details>


### [248] [Ontology-Guided Knowledge Graph Construction and Robust Evidence Retrieval for Indian Knowledge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14050)
*Anand Kaniyaar Sriranga Prasad,Avinash Holenarasipur Nagendra,Mohan Raghavan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出本体引导的知识图谱检索流水线，在印度知识系统证据检索中牺牲速度换精度：独立KG在Precision@5与多跳MRR上最优，KG+SV+Entity Notes联合则Recall@20最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度知识系统的证据检索面临音译变体、名称歧义、关系跨段落分散三大难题，单一向量检索难以胜任，需要类型化实体-关系的结构化组织与原文证据可追溯的检索方案。

Method: 构建本体引导的KG流水线：用Qwen3-32B抽取类型化实体与关系并链接到原文证据；检索端融合sense-aware grounding（义项感知接地）、有界图遍历、词汇锚点、关系向量检索与段落重排。在Dhara Bench的320题上，控制逐题候选数一致（BGE与Qwen两种重排器），比较独立KG、单向量检索(SV)、KG∪SV、KG∪SV∪Entity Notes四种内部配置，并与Gemini 3.7 Flash及部署计时对照。

Result: 独立KG在两种重排器下Precision@5最高；Qwen重排下Counting/Composite/Multi-hop的MRR@20最优，Multi-hop达0.8722（全重排KG+SV为0.7622）；KG+SV+Entity Notes的Recall@20最高为0.5134（KG为0.4347）。15道多跳题上Qwen KG完整证据MRR@20为0.4651（SV为0.3372），两种全联合在20条结果内完成10题（KG为9题）。Gemini 3.7 Flash（人工核验源映射）整体MRR与nDCG仍更强，内部系统存在类别特异性优势。所有比较均为描述性；平均检索时延KG 20.374 s、SV 0.646 s。

Conclusion: KG与向量检索呈精度—召回互补：独立KG利于早期相关性与多跳推理，联合方案利于证据完整性，但时延成本高约30倍。应按“早期相关性/证据完整性/检索代价”的权衡选择配置，而非追求单一最优系统。

Abstract: Retrieving evidence from Indian Knowledge Systems is complicated by transliteration variants, ambiguous names, and relations distributed across passages. We present an ontology-guided knowledge graph (KG) pipeline that links typed entities and relations extracted with Qwen3-32B to original textual evidence. Retrieval combines sense-aware grounding, bounded graph traversal, lexical anchors, relationship-vector search, and passage reranking. We evaluate KG alone, single-vector retrieval (SV), and KG unions with SV and Entity Notes on 320 questions from Dhara Bench, matching the per-question candidate count across internal systems under BGE and Qwen rerankers. Standalone KG has the highest Precision@5 under both rerankers and the highest MRR@20 in Counting, Composite, and Multi-hop among the evaluated internal configurations. With Qwen, KG achieves Multi-hop MRR@20 of 0.8722, compared with 0.7622 for the fully reranked KG + SV union. The KG + SV + Entity Notes union instead provides the highest overall Recall@20 (0.5134), compared with 0.4347 for KG. On the 15 Multi-hop questions, Qwen KG achieves complete-evidence MRR@20 of 0.4651 versus 0.3372 for SV, while both full unions complete ten questions within twenty results compared with nine for KG. Gemini 3.7 Flash, evaluated through manually reviewed source mappings, re mains stronger on overall MRR and nDCG, while internal systems have category-specific advantages. All comparisons are descriptive. A separate deployment timing study measures mean retrieval times of 20.374 s for KG and 0.646 s for SV. The findings support selecting KG or its unions according to the required balance of early relevance, evidence completeness, and retrieval cost.

</details>


### [249] [From Search to Decision: How Post-Training Shapes Information-Seeking Capabilities in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14149)
*Haohang Huang,Xuan Lu,Mingjin Kuai,Yuxuan Zhang,Wei Ji,Haiyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文围绕大语言模型信息寻求行为的六项相关能力展开综述，系统梳理其学习信号、优化方法、更新位置与信用分配机制，并总结实证进展、评估实践与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统的信息寻求（何时获取信息、何处检索、如何评估与使用证据）日益重要；近期后训练方法虽改进了若干此类行为，但目标与评估分散于不同任务与系统设定中，缺乏统一组织框架。

Method: 综述性/框架性研究：提出六项相互关联的信息寻求能力的组织框架，并对每项能力按学习信号、优化方法、更新位置（update loci）和信用分配机制四个维度进行归类与分析，同时综合目标行为、端到端性能、搜索控制与泛化方面的实证进展。

Result: 现有研究报告了在检索路由、查询精炼、多工具探索、证据支撑推理和资源感知搜索等方面的改进，但各能力的行为证据覆盖范围不一，进展不均衡。

Conclusion: 识别出三项开放挑战——估计前瞻性检索效用、协调信息寻求决策、适应陌生检索环境；并讨论评估实践以支撑对未来进展的解读。

Abstract: Information seeking in large language model (LLM) systems increasingly involves decisions about when to acquire information, where to search, and how to evaluate and use evidence. Recent post-training methods have improved several such behaviors, but their objectives and evaluations span different tasks and system settings. This paper organizes research around six interrelated information-seeking capabilities and examines the learning signals, optimization methods, update loci, and credit-assignment mechanisms used to develop them. We synthesize empirical progress in targeted behaviors, end-to-end performance, search control, and generalization. Existing studies report gains in retrieval routing, query refinement, multi-tool exploration, evidence-grounded reasoning, and resource-aware search, although the scope of behavioral evidence varies across capabilities. We identify open challenges in estimating prospective retrieval utility, coordinating information-seeking decisions, and adapting to unfamiliar retrieval environments, and discuss evaluation practices for interpreting future progress.

</details>


### [250] [SHARP: Semantic Hierarchical Agentic Reasoning for Next POI Prediction with Dynamic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14220)
*Yuhang Zhan,Silin Zhou,Zhuo Chen,Haoxiang Lei,Yunren Feng,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SHARP：一个agentic分层框架，用类别级语义推理引导具体POI预测，结合动态轨迹记忆库、多粒度时间感知类别转移图与RQ-VAE语义ID，端到端联合优化，在三个真实数据集上Acc@5/Acc@10平均提升5.29/4.52个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽结合LLM语义推理与图结构建模集体移动性，但仍存在三个问题：(1) LLM语义推理与具体POI预测之间存在鸿沟；(2) 用户移动性随时间演化，需在有限LLM上下文内保留并更新历史知识；(3) 全局转移图会抹平特定时段的类别转移模式。

Method: 提出SHARP框架：(a) 轨迹记忆agent维护动态轨迹记忆库，在上下文增长受限下保留早期移动模式并追踪行为变化；(b) 构建多粒度时间感知类别转移图（全局视图+时间条件视图），保留被全局聚合掩盖的时间规律；(c) 类别预测agent融合上述图与轨迹记忆预测下一类别；(d) 通过RQ-VAE学习语义ID构建语义POI表示，与类别引导的POI预测器端到端联合优化，实现类别级推理引导细粒度POI预测的分层agentic结构。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上，相较最强基线，Acc@5平均提升5.29个百分点，Acc@10平均提升4.52个百分点。

Conclusion: 将语义推理与POI预测解耦为"类别级推理→具体POI预测"的层次化agentic流程，辅以动态记忆与时间条件图，可有效弥补LLM语义与细粒度预测间的鸿沟并刻画移动性的时序演化，验证了分层语义推理范式的有效性。

Abstract: Next point-of-interest (POI) prediction supports personalized location services by anticipating users' next destinations. Recent methods leverage large language models (LLMs) for semantic reasoning and graphs for collective mobility modeling, improving POI prediction. However, three challenges still remain. Bridging LLMs' semantic reasoning and specific POI prediction remains challenging. User mobility also evolves over time, making it necessary to retain and update historical knowledge within limited LLM contexts. Meanwhile, global transition graphs smooth out time-specific category transition patterns. To address these challenges, we propose Semantic Hierarchical Agentic Reasoning for Next POI Prediction with Dynamic Memory (SHARP), an agentic hierarchical framework in which category-level reasoning guides specific POI prediction. A trajectory memory agent maintains a dynamic trajectory memory bank that preserves earlier mobility patterns and tracks behavioral changes with limited context growth. We construct multi-granularity time-aware category transition graphs with global and time-conditioned views to preserve temporal regularities obscured by global aggregation. A category predict agent combines these graphs with trajectory memory to predict likely next categories. SHARP learns semantic IDs (SIDs) through a residual-quantized variational autoencoder (RQ-VAE) to construct semantic POI representations, which are jointly optimized with a category-guided POI predictor end-to-end. On three real-world datasets, SHARP improves Acc@5 and Acc@10 by an average of 5.29 and 4.52 percentage points, respectively, over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [251] [FERN: Cross-Benchmark Table Retrieval for Text-to-SQL with Frozen Retrievers and Learned Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14477)
*Arjun Rao,Nishant Gopinath*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FERN 是一种不使用生成式 LLM 的表格检索方法：融合三个冻结检索器得到 100 个候选表，用最多 120 维特征描述每个候选，再用一次性在 BIRD 训练集上拟合的 LightGBM 排序器重排。它在 Spider 上以较小延迟达到或超过 RASL 的召回表现，但在 BIRD（无证据提示）、BEAVER 与 Fiben 上仍存在差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模合并目录上的 Text-to-SQL 系统必须先检索出问题所需的表格。已有方法 RASL 依赖 Claude 3.5 等生成式模型抽取关键词并选表，成本与延迟较高。作者希望验证：不依赖生成式 LLM 的特征工程排序方法能否在表格检索召回上与之竞争。

Method: FERN（Feature-Engineered Ranking with No generative LLM）：将三个冻结检索器融合成 100 个候选表；为每个候选构造最多 120 个特征；用 LightGBM 排序器重排，排序器仅在 BIRD 训练问题上拟合一次，不调用托管模型。评测在 pooled BIRD、Spider、Fiben 目录及 121 条真实 BEAVER 问题上进行，与 RASL 公布的表格召回对比。

Result: Spider（未拟合）：候选池单独在前 15 表即达到 RASL 公布召回（98.0）；排序器将 complete@5 从 83.9% 提升至 95.6%。前 5 表召回：Spider 96.7 vs 97.0；BIRD 含证据提示 96.6 vs 97.5；BIRD 无证据提示低 8.2 点（89.3）。BEAVER（未拟合）：前 15 表召回 82.1，介于两个 BEAVER 训练检索器（80.8、84.5）之间，但 complete@15 更低（49.6 vs 54.5、62.0）。Fiben：前 15 表明显落后（54.2 vs 69.2，问题集不同），前 50 表为 80.7 vs 79.4。BIRD dev 消融：非检索类特征使 complete@5 相对纯检索信号提升 7.7 点。延迟：单张 A100 上每题中位 208 ms。

Conclusion: 不用生成式 LLM、仅靠冻结检索器融合与特征工程排序，即可在部分设置（如 Spider）达到甚至超越 RASL 的表格召回，且推理成本低、延迟可控；但跨数据集泛化仍受限，在 BIRD 无证据提示、BEAVER 全表覆盖（complete@15）与 Fiben 上存在明显差距，说明排序器对训练分布与问题/证据形式较敏感。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems over large pooled catalogs must first retrieve the tables a question needs. We compare against the published table recall of RASL, which uses Claude 3.5 models to extract keywords and select tables, on pooled BIRD, Spider, and Fiben catalogs. FERN (Feature-Engineered Ranking with No generative LLM) instead fuses three frozen retrievers into 100 candidates, describes each with up to 120 features, and reorders them with LightGBM rankers fitted once on BIRD's training questions. On Spider, where it was not fitted, its candidate pool alone reaches RASL's published recall at fifteen tables (98.0), and the ranker raises the share of questions with every gold table in the top five (complete@5) from 83.9% to 95.6%. At five tables, FERN's recall is within one point of RASL's on Spider (96.7 vs 97.0) and on BIRD with its evidence hints (96.6 vs 97.5), and 8.2 points below on BIRD without them (89.3). On 121 real BEAVER questions, without BEAVER fitting, its recall at fifteen (82.1) falls between two BEAVER-trained retrievers (80.8, 84.5), but its complete@15 is below both (49.6 vs 54.5 and 62.0). On Fiben, it falls far short at fifteen tables (54.2 vs 69.2, different question sets), although its recall at fifty is numerically close to RASL's question-only retrieval result (80.7 vs 79.4). On BIRD dev with evidence, non-retrieval features raise complete@5 by 7.7 points over retrieval signals alone. On BIRD dev, median latency is 208 ms per question on one A100, without hosted model calls.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Understanding Computing Instructors' Perceptions of Student Sense of Belonging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13237)
*Ritesh Kanchi,Teresa Luo,Sam Grunebaum,Alex Oesterling*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过对美国大型公立大学九位入门计算课程教师进行半结构化访谈，揭示了教师如何界定、测量、构建学生归属感，以及所用干预措施和面临的多层障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究强调教师对学生归属感的影响，但鲜有研究考察计算学科教师如何感知、理解并尝试支持学生归属感，因此需要填补这一视角空白。

Method: 采用定性研究，对美国大型公立大学九位入门计算课程教师开展半结构化访谈，围绕归属感的定义与测量、干预策略及障碍三个问题进行分析。

Result: 教师从价值与身份代表性、自我效能感、共同体三方面界定归属感；依据问卷、观察和学生成绩进行测量；认为归属感受先前经历、专业、学生与院校文化及代表性影响；实施了面向学生的干预并围绕归属感设计课程；同时识别出多层级支持障碍。

Conclusion: 研究为设计兼顾既有研究与教师认知的归属感干预提供机会，并提示需关注助教使用、学生期望平衡及适应影响归属感的社会趋势等张力。

Abstract: Students' sense of belonging has been described as a feeling of community acceptance, respect, inclusion, and membership. In computing, student belonging is shaped by psychological, social, academic, and environmental factors, including their relationships with instructors. However, despite prior work emphasizing instructors' influence on student belonging, limited research has examined how computing instructors perceive, understand, and attempt to support belonging. Through nine semi-structured qualitative interviews with introductory computing instructors at large public universities in the United States, we investigated how instructors define, measure, and conceptualize belonging; what interventions they use to support student belonging; and what barriers limit the efficacy of these interventions. Our findings show that instructors defined belonging through value and identity representation, self-efficacy, and community; relied on surveys, observations, and student performance to measure belonging; saw belonging to be influenced by prior experience, major, student and institutional culture, and representation; implemented student-oriented interventions and designed courses around belonging; and identified multi-level challenges to supporting belonging. These results highlight opportunities to design belonging interventions that attend to both prior work and instructor conceptualizations, while also considering tensions in utilizing teaching assistants, balancing students' expectations, and adapting to broader societal trends affecting belonging.

</details>


### [253] [Developing an Operational Model for an Internet of Data Based Trusted Data Space for Vocational Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13520)
*Yalan Dong,Hao Tian,Hongyang Jia,Chunxue Wang,Wenchang Lei*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 基于数据互联网构建职业教育可信数据空间，并提出涵盖价值网络、产品组合、收益分配与治理规则的多主体运营模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业教育数据流通长期受制于权属不清、信任缺失与激励不足，亟需可信的跨域流通底层技术与可持续运营机制。

Method: 在数据互联网支撑的职业教育可信数据空间基础上，设计多主体运营模型，包含价值网络、产品组合、收益分配与治理规则四个模块，突出数据主权保护与激励相容。

Result: 形成一套面向区域职业教育数据生态的多主体运营框架，为数据主权保护与激励相容提供机制设计参考（摘要未给出实证或量化结果）。

Conclusion: 该运营模型可为区域职业教育数据生态的可持续运营提供参考，尚需进一步实证验证其可操作性与激励效果。

Abstract: Data circulation in vocational education has long been constrained by unclear ownership, a lack of trust, and insufficient incentives. The Internet of Data provides a new underlying technology for enabling trusted cross-domain data circulation. Building on an Internet of Data based trusted data space for vocational education, this paper develops a multi-stakeholder operational model encompassing a value network, a product portfolio, revenue allocation, and governance rules. Emphasizing data sovereignty protection and incentive compatibility, the model provides a reference for the sustainable operation of regional vocational education data ecosystems.

</details>


### [254] [Repertoires of Contention: Building computational tools and infrastructures for community resistance to bitcoin mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13527)
*Marina Johnson-Zafiris,Owen Marshall,Jen Liu,Joseph Ferdinando,Steven J. Jackson,Chris Csíkszentmihályi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过多种计算技术参与纽约州北部Greenidge比特币矿电厂的社区争议，旨在产出批判性环境知识并培育"计算支持的抗争剧目"，以推动更具政治参与性的HCI研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI及相关领域已有将社区成员作为共同设计者、协作者与决策者以挑战结构性权力壁垒的取向；本文进一步聚焦环境争议中知识—权力的不平等关系（如监管惰性与热污染问题），探索计算技术如何服务于社区抗争。

Method: 与社区团体协作，针对监管惰性、热水污染等具体问题，应用多样化计算技术（computational techniques）进行参与式、介入式研究，建构社区可用的争议与行动手段。

Result: 形成了新的批判性环境知识，并发展出"计算支持的抗争剧目"（computationally-supported repertoires of contention），即用以质疑和挑战不平等知识—权力关系的手段集合。

Conclusion: 此类社区参与式计算实践可支撑更具政治参与性的HCI形态，通过战略性社会技术干预与联盟，指向对财富、知识与影响力差距的纠正与修复。

Abstract: Recent work in human-computer interaction and allied fields has sought to engage community members as co-designers, facilitators, and decision makers to challenge structural power barriers and knowledge gaps. In this paper, we apply a diverse set of computational techniques to engage and address community concerns surrounding the socio-environmental impacts of the Greenidge Bitcoin mining power plant facility in upstate New York. Through collaborative interactions with community groups, addressing problems ranging from regulatory inertia to thermal water pollution, we aim to facilitate not just the production of new and critical environmental knowledge but also new computationally-supported repertoires of contention: means of contesting the unequal relations of knowledge and power that structure and often determine the outcomes of environmental controversies. We argue such engagements can help support more politically engaged forms of HCI, oriented towards efforts to redress and repair disparities in wealth, knowledge, and influence through strategic sociotechnical interventions and alliances.

</details>


### [255] [Perspicacious: An Educational Intelligence Platform for Instructional Designers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13549)
*Yumou Wei,Michael C Sutton,Paulo F. Carvalho*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了"Perspicacious"教育智能平台，将题目生成与知识组件（KC）发现整合于统一界面，以弥合AI教育研究到教学设计师实践之间的鸿沟。基于17次生产运行的成本模型显示，从576题题库提取KC的成本为1.98–4.11美元、耗时36–158分钟；八名教学设计师的形成性研究显示SUS均值78.8，并归纳出部署前的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教学设计师需围绕明确界定的知识组件（KC）设计评估题目，但该过程耗时且高度依赖专家劳动。尽管AI教育领域已有自动题目生成与KC发现方法，却少有转化为教学设计师可直接使用的易用工具，形成研究与实践的鸿沟。

Method: 开发名为"Perspicacious"的教育智能平台，在单一界面下统一题目生成与KC发现；用17次生产运行校准成本模型以刻画成本—时间权衡；并开展八名教学设计师参与的形成性、部分"绿野仙踪"（Wizard-of-Oz）研究，结合SUS量表与实际需求采集。

Result: 成本模型显示：从576题题库中提取KC花费1.98–4.11美元，耗时36–158分钟，随预算不同而变化。用户研究显示感知可用性较强（SUS均值78.8），并识别出作为部署前提的具体需求。

Conclusion: 将题目生成与KC发现整合为面向使用者的平台可有效衔接研究与教学实践；成本与耗时在可接受范围内且可按预算调节；平台具备良好感知可用性，但需满足研究者归纳的若干具体需求方可用于正式部署。

Abstract: Designing effective instruction requires instructional designers to create assessment questions aligned with clearly defined knowledge components (KCs). These KCs represent specific knowledge or skills that students must acquire. This process is often time-consuming and relies heavily on expert labor. Although AI for education research has produced a growing body of automated question-generation and KC-discovery methods, few have moved beyond research prototypes into user-friendly tools that instructional designers can readily use. To bridge the research-to-practice gap, we developed and evaluated an educational intelligence platform that unifies question generation and KC discovery behind a single interface to support instructional content development. Named "Perspicacious," our platform aims to help instructional designers "see through" complex data and algorithms to derive instructional insights. A cost model calibrated on 17 production runs revealed a cost-time trade-off: extracting KCs from a 576-question bank cost US $1.98-4.11 and took 36-158 minutes to complete, depending on the budget. A formative, partially Wizard-of-Oz study with eight instructional designers found strong perceived usability (mean SUS 78.8) and surfaced concrete requirements that we treat as prerequisites for deployment.

</details>


### [256] [Transparency Disclosures for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13873)
*Jacob Davies,Theo Bearman*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文主张前沿AI开发者应披露五项指标，以使思维链（CoT）可监控性所受威胁可追踪并促进协调，并对Anthropic、OpenAI和Google DeepMind的现有披露进行审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控是监控先进AI系统的重要工具，但正面临威胁；需要可追踪的指标与跨开发者协调来维护CoT可监控性。

Method: 沿着CoT监控所依赖的五步链条，识别五个高相关且披露可行的指标；对三家前沿开发者的公开披露进行审计。

Result: 没有任何开发者覆盖全部指标集，但每个指标都至少被一家前沿开发者报告过；现有披露主要依赖开发者自评。

Conclusion: 建议进行跨开发者审计，采用比公开披露更深的访问权限，可由嵌入开发者内部的评估者或独立验证组织执行。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is one of the most valuable tools for monitoring advanced AI systems, but it is under threat. We argue that frontier AI developers should report specific indicators to make these threats trackable and to better facilitate coordination to preserve CoT monitorability. We identify five indicators that are both highly relevant and reasonably feasible to disclose, one for each link in a five-step chain on which CoT monitoring depends. The first, third-party CoT access and base architecture specifications, also underpins external verification of the rest. The others span CoT quality (naturalness and faithfulness) and monitoring effectiveness (detection and uplift). An audit of current disclosures by Anthropic, OpenAI, and Google DeepMind shows that no developer covers the full set, but that every indicator has been reported by at least one frontier developer. Because current disclosures rely largely on developers' own evaluations, we also recommend that a cross-developer audit be repeated with deeper access than public disclosures allow, either by embedded evaluators working within developers or by an Independent Verification Organization.

</details>


### [257] [Advancing a Global Framework for AI Safety and Governance for the Well-being of Humanity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13968)
*Zeng Yi,Seán Ó hÉigeartaigh,Wang Zhengqi,Lu Enmeng,Chen Yu,Cao Gongce,Guo Xiaoyang,Kang Yanrong,Han Kaiyu,Fan Jinyu,Xie Jiawei,Han Zhengqiang,Wang Jin,Huangfu Cunqing,Bao Aorigele,Dame Wendy Hall,Lin Lin,Duan Weiwen,Wang Rong,Zhang Junlin,Tang Xinhua,Vincent C. Müller,Chen Jingjing,Li Na,Cheng Kai,Sebastian Sunday Grève,Danil Kerimi,Bernd Holznagel,Anna Abramova,Jimena Sofia Viveros Alvarez,Edson Prestes,Shen Juncheng,Liu Yongmou,Zhang Jiji,Nada Laabidi,Yao Xin,Zhou Kai,George Chen,Fu Chunhui,Liu Xiaochun,Guo Sumin,Hu Naying,Qiao Qian,Meng Wei,Zhang Linghan,Tan Zhixing,Helen Meng,Qiao Yu,Bu Yuyan,Fan Wei,Wang Xingguang,Wang Feng,Gong Xinqi,Xia Wenhui,Wang Mengyin,Tao Feng,Hu Yongqi,Charuka Senal Damunupola,Cheng Ming,Liu Weichen,Nirosha Ananda,Peng Tao,Tao Tao,Wang Fengqiong,Wang Jianbing,Wang Tong,Wang Wei,Wang Xin,Wu Shuyan,Yang Yaodong,Yang Zhongliang,John Yeoh,Zhang Rong,Zhou Yuan,Zhang Xueli,Liang Hao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该报告系统梳理全球AI安全与治理的探索与实践，识别关键问题，借鉴多边治理经验，以机制设计为中心，提出构建以联合国为中心的全球AI安全与治理框架的制度安排与政策建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全球AI安全与治理探索分散、关键问题尚未解决，缺少统一权威的全球治理机制，需借鉴其他领域多边治理经验以推进体系建设。

Method: 文献与实践的系统性梳理；关键问题识别；跨领域多边治理经验类比借鉴；以机制设计为中心的框架构建与政策建议提出。

Result: 提出兼顾安全、包容、责任清晰、协调有效、权威高效的全球AI安全与治理体系设计，并给出以联合国为中心的治理框架的制度性措施与政策建议。

Conclusion: 该报告可为政府、国际组织、企业与研究机构提供参考，推动以人为中心、有益于人类的全球AI治理，确保AI技术真正服务于全人类福祉。

Abstract: The Report "Advancing a Global Framework for AI Safety and Governance for the Well-being of Humanity" is an important outcome of the AI Safety and Governance Program of World Internet Conference Specialized Committee on Artificial Intelligence in 2025. This report provides a systematic review of current global explorations and practices in AI safety and governance, identifies key issues that need to be addressed, and draws upon multilateral governance experiences from other domains. Centered on mechanism design, it explores how to build a global AI safety and governance system that ensures safety and security, promotes inclusiveness, clarifies responsibilities and accountabilities, strengthens effective coordination, and ensures authoritative and efficient governance. Guided by the principles of multilateralism and the vision of building a community with a shared future for humankind, the report proposes institutional measures and policy recommendations to establish a United Nations-centered global AI safety and governance framework. It aims to serve as a reference for governments, international organizations, enterprises, and research institutions. The report is to contribute insights and momentum to advancing people-centered and beneficial AI governance worldwide, ensuring that AI technologies truly serve the well-being of all humanity.

</details>


### [258] [Can AI be a 'Researcher Without Borders'? Auditing Bias in AI-Assisted Social Science Research about China and the US](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14288)
*Yuan Zhang,Thales Bertaglia,Ralph Schroeder*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究构建基准任务，让AI就涉美、涉华及一般社会科学问题撰写短文，考察US/中国/欧洲模型的国别偏向与引用来源差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在算法与数学任务上表现优异，但作为社会科学研究助手的适用性不明，因为许多社科问题缺乏公认的可量化标准答案；随着研究者与学生日益依赖AI生成研究设想，若模型对开发国或其学术传统存在偏向，将扭曲研究发现并损害科学可信度与公共传播。

Method: 设计基准任务：要求AI就涉美、涉华的社会科学问题及若干更广泛的社科问题撰写短论文。操纵三个条件：问题类型（跨国比较、国别特定、一般问题）、模型（2个美国开发、2个中国开发、1个欧洲开发）、提示方式（基础写作请求、研究者身份、美国研究者身份、中国研究者身份）。由两名具备中美社科研究经验的研究者人工标注偏向与来源使用。

Result: 总体上，美、中、欧模型均未表现出政治偏向，但在引用来源上存在差异；部分比较性问题仍偏向某一国家，且这种倾向在某些问题上更为明显。

Conclusion: 模型在多国问题上的偏向并非普遍性政治偏向，而更多体现为来源选择差异与个别比较题的偏向；研究者与学生在将AI用于社会科学研究时应保持审慎。

Abstract: Although large language models have demonstrated strong performance on algorithmic and mathematical tasks, their suitability as social science research assistants remains unclear, particularly because many social science questions lack agreed-upon, quantifiable benchmark answers. Evaluating this suitability is important since researchers and students increasingly rely on AI to generate research ideas. When questions concern the countries in which models were developed, bias may be exacerbated: Do AI models favour the countries where they were developed or the social theories associated with those countries' scholarly traditions? Such biases could distort research findings, undermine scientific credibility, and distort findings when they are disseminated to the public. To investigate whether models exhibit country-focused bias in social science research, we designed a benchmarking task requiring AI to write short essays on social science questions in relation to the US and China as well as some broader social science questions. We varied three conditions: question type (cross-country comparisons, country-specific questions, and general questions); model (two US-developed, two China-developed, and one Europe-developed model); and prompting modality (a base essay-writing request, a researcher identity, a US researcher identity, and a Chinese researcher identity). Responses were manually annotated for bias and source use by two researchers with experience in social science in relation to China and the US. Our analyses show that, on the whole, US and Chinese models and the European one are not politically biased but differ in the sources they cite. Still, some comparative questions favour one country over another, and this tendency is more pronounced for certain questions. These findings suggest that researchers and students should exercise caution when using AI for social science research.

</details>
